# Consensus rules of hayai

Each consensus rule of the ZIPs and of the
[Zcash protocol specification](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf) has one row: the rule,
the hayai code that enforces it, and the test that checks it. The Code column links to the line of
the check. When hayai does not implement a rule, the Code column gives the reason why the node is
correct without it, or the open issue. `scripts/consensus_links.py` points the links at the
current tree after a change of the code: it finds the text of each linked line in its new
file and lists the links whose code is gone.

✅ implemented · ⚠️ partly implemented (at least one rule open) · ❌ not implemented yet

The Zakura column describes Zakura commit [`8456a42`](https://github.com/zakura-core/zakura/tree/8456a42c21f2fd268b5c9701494f8cdf491906dd). The zebrad column describes Zebra commit [`e3eef2f`](https://github.com/ZcashFoundation/zebra/tree/e3eef2f37c35127ad1769f19a1ebc7eaa5d5d291), the release v6.4.2.

## [ZIP 143](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0143): Transaction Signature Validation for Overwinter

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A v3 transaction uses the signature hash of ZIP 143. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L212), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (only Overwinter blocks hold v3 transactions; the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_version_before_sapling_is_not_verified`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1891) |
| From Overwinter, the v1 and v2 transaction formats are not valid. | [`TxVersions::allows`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198), [`zcash_primitives::TxVersion::read`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L82), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers the heights from Overwinter to Canopy) | [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682) |
| The signature hash commits to the consensus branch id of the epoch. | [`PrepareError::BranchId`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L206), [`zcash_primitives::consensus_branch_id`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash_v4.rs#L142), [`zcash_primitives::consensus_branch_id`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/txid.rs#L241), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |

## [ZIP 155](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0155): addrv2 message

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| An `addrv2` entry holds the time, the services as CompactSize, the network id, the address length as CompactSize, the address, and the port in big-endian order. | [`Reader::addr_v2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L884), [`LegacyMessage::AddrV2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L473) | [`addrv2_vectors_keep_ipv4_and_ipv6_and_drop_other_networks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L368), [`addrv2_encodes_ipv4_and_ipv6`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L497) |
| The port of an `addrv2` entry is 0 when the network has no port. | Not implemented: hayai sends no `addrv2` message. | none |
| A node refuses an `addrv2` message with more than 1,000 addresses. | [`MAX_ADDR_ENTRIES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L633), [`MAX_ADDR_ENTRIES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L43) | [`addrv2_rejects_invalid_lengths`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L438) |
| A node refuses an `addrv2` address field of more than 512 bytes, whatever the network id. | [`MAX_ADDRV2_ADDR_LEN`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L890), [`MAX_ADDRV2_ADDR_LEN`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L45) | [`addrv2_rejects_invalid_lengths`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L438) |
| No node uses the network id 0x03 (Tor v2). | [`ADDRV2_IPV4`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L900) (the decoder drops every network id other than IPv4 and IPv6; hayai sends no `addrv2` message) | [`addrv2_vectors_keep_ipv4_and_ipv6_and_drop_other_networks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L368) |
| A node gossips the valid addresses of all known networks (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai keeps only IPv4 and IPv6 addresses and does not gossip Tor, I2P or CJDNS addresses, and no block or transaction verdict depends on them. | [`addrv2_vectors_keep_ipv4_and_ipv6_and_drop_other_networks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L368) |
| A node does not gossip the addresses of unknown networks. | [`ADDRV2_IPV4`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L900) (the decoder drops an entry of an unknown network) | [`addrv2_vectors_keep_ipv4_and_ipv6_and_drop_other_networks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L368) |
| A node refuses an `addrv2` message with an address whose length is not the length of its known network id. | [`DecodeError::AddrV2Length`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L912) (IPv4 and IPv6 only: a TORV3, I2P or CJDNS entry of a wrong length is dropped, not refused; issue hayai-00t) | [`addrv2_rejects_invalid_lengths`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L438) |
| A node sends a Tor v3 address with the network id `TORV3` and the 32-byte key. | Not implemented: hayai sends no Tor address. | none |
| A node sends an I2P address with the network id `I2P` and the 32-byte hash. | Not implemented: hayai sends no I2P address. | none |
| A node sends a CJDNS address with the network id `CJDNS`. | Not implemented: hayai sends no CJDNS address. | none |
| A node does not send `addrv2` to a peer below the deployment version. | [`LegacyMessage::Addr`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1402), [`LegacyMessage::Addr`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1419) (hayai sends `addr` only) | none |
| A node handles a received `addr` message as before. | [`Relay::on_addr`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1375) | [`zebra_addr_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L123), [`a_fresh_unsolicited_address_goes_on_to_two_peers_once`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/peers.rs#L1091) |

## [ZIP 200](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0200): Network Upgrade Mechanism

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| Each network upgrade has a unique nonzero 32-bit `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID`. | [`Upgrade::branch_id`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L522), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L784), [`nu7_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-crypto/src/lib.rs#L80) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988), [`every_upgrade_with_a_branch_id_has_one_rule_set_with_its_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L149) |
| The branch id 0 can mark the Sprout rules (option). | [`BranchId::Sprout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L524), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId::Sprout`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L787) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| The `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of an upgrade is not zero. | [`SpecError::Order`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L305), [`RegtestConfigError::Order`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L287) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988), [`a_configured_regtest_has_its_heights_and_its_checkpoints`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L1081) |
| An epoch starts at the `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of its upgrade and stops before the next one, so the block at `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT - 1` has the old rules. | [`Network::upgrade_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L882), [`rules_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L105) | [`the_rule_set_changes_at_every_activation_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L190), [`the_upgrade_changes_at_every_activation_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L1045) |
| The `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` is above the `DEPRECATION_HEIGHT` of the last release without the upgrade. | Not implemented: the rule binds the release process of a deployment, not a node. | none |
| The `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` is about 3 months after the first release with the upgrade (recommendation). | Not implemented: the rule binds the release process of a deployment, not a node. | none |
| A changed `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` gets a new `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID`. | Not implemented: the rule binds the authors of a deployment ZIP, not a node. | none |
| A block-height check gates each rule of an upgrade. | [`rules_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L105) | [`the_rule_set_changes_at_every_activation_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L190), [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |
| A node validates a block of known height under the rules of the branch of that height. | [`rules_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L1829), [`RawBlock::parse`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L252), [`PrepareError::BranchId`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L206) | [`the_rule_set_changes_at_every_activation_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L190), [`the_rules_at_the_nu7_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/params.rs#L136) |
| A node can keep a block of unknown height until its parents arrive (option). | Not implemented: the node refuses the block and gets the parent through the header synchronization. | none |
| A reorganization across an activation height follows the normal reorganization rules. | [`most_work`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L662), [`rules_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L1829) | none |
| A node that upgrades late stops and gives an alert when it sees many invalid blocks (recommendation). | Not implemented: the node refuses each invalid block and continues, and no verdict depends on the alert. | none |
| Below the `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT`, the mempool does not accept a transaction that is valid only after it (recommendation). | [`rules_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/admission.rs#L180), [`PolicyReject::Epoch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L292) | [`the_epoch_is_the_epoch_of_the_next_block`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L568), [`a_chain_crosses_two_upgrades_at_the_tip_and_during_the_synchronization`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/sync_tests.rs#L1473) |
| At the `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT`, the mempool removes the transactions that are never valid after it (recommendation). | [`set_epoch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L1244), [`Mempool::still_prepared`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/admission.rs#L245) | [`a_chain_crosses_two_upgrades_at_the_tip_and_during_the_synchronization`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/sync_tests.rs#L1473) |
| From Overwinter, a signature commits to the `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` (two-way replay protection). | [`PrepareError::BranchId`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L206), [`zcash_primitives::consensus_branch_id`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash_v4.rs#L142), [`zcash_primitives::consensus_branch_id`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/txid.rs#L241) | [`sighash_vector_transactions_through_draft`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_txs.rs#L267) |

## [ZIP 201](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0201): Network Peer Management for Overwinter

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A node refuses a peer whose version is below `MIN_PEER_PROTO_VERSION` (170,002). | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484), [`a_peer_below_the_minimum_protocol_version_is_refused_without_a_ban`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/peers.rs#L730) |
| The Overwinter constants are `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 170,003 (Testnet) and 170,005 (Mainnet), and `MIN_PEER_PROTO_VERSION` 170,002. | [`min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L62), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum 170,150 refuses every peer before NU6.2) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| In the 1,728 blocks before an activation, a node disconnects the peers without the upgrade first (`NETWORK_UPGRADE_PEER_PREFERENCE_BLOCK_PERIOD`). | Not implemented: a full inbound set refuses a new connection, and no block or transaction verdict depends on the choice of peers. | none |
| After the activation, a node refuses new connections from peers without the upgrade. | [`min_peer_version_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L1857), [`min_peer_version_at`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L2629), [`min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1208) | [`a_peer_below_the_version_of_the_upgrade_of_the_tip_is_disconnected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L3347) |
| After the activation, a node disconnects the connected peers without the upgrade. | [`Relay::set_min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L767), [`Relay::set_min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L1258), [`Relay::set_min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L1569) | [`a_peer_below_the_version_of_the_upgrade_of_the_tip_is_disconnected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node.rs#L3347) |
| A node sends `reject` with `REJECT_OBSOLETE` before it disconnects an obsolete peer. | Not implemented: the peer gets no reason for the disconnection, and no block or transaction verdict depends on it. | none |
| The Overwinter `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` is 0x5ba81b19, and the `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` is 207,500 on Testnet and 347,500 on Mainnet. | [`BranchId::Overwinter`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L525), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId::Overwinter`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L788), [`SpecError::Order`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L305), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Overwinter`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L493), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Overwinter`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L528) | [`activation_heights_match_zcash_protocol`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L943), [`every_upgrade_with_a_branch_id_has_one_rule_set_with_its_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L149) |

## [ZIP 202](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0202): Version 3 Transaction Format for Overwinter

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| An Overwinter transaction has the version 3, the version group 0x03C48270 and the flag `fOverwintered` set. | [`zcash_primitives::TxVersion::V3`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L89), [`V3_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L118), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| A node refuses a transaction without `fOverwintered`, with an unknown version group or with an unknown version. | [`zcash_primitives::TxVersion::read`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L82), [`TxVersions::allows`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198) | [`random_bytes_never_panic`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L506), [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682) |
| The validation of a v3 transaction uses the signature process of ZIP 143. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L212), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_version_before_sapling_is_not_verified`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1891) |

## [ZIP 203](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0203): Transaction Expiry

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The expiry height `nExpiryHeight` of a transaction is at most 499,999,999. | [`TX_EXPIRY_HEIGHT_THRESHOLD`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L483) | [`an_expiry_height_is_below_the_threshold`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L966) |
| A transaction with a nonzero expiry height is not valid in a block above its expiry height. | [`ContextError::Expired`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L579) | [`expiry_and_lock_time_rules`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L384) |
| Before NU5, the expiry height of a coinbase transaction has no rule. | [`CoinbaseRules::expiry_is_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L399) | [`coinbase_placement_and_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L787) |
| From NU5, the `nExpiryHeight` of a coinbase transaction equals the block height. | [`ContextError::CoinbaseExpiry`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L400) | [`coinbase_placement_and_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L787) |
| From NU5, the bound of 499,999,999 does not apply to a coinbase transaction. | [`TX_EXPIRY_HEIGHT_THRESHOLD`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L483) (the bound applies to every transaction; from NU5 the expiry of a coinbase is its height, which is always below the bound, so the verdict is the same) | [`an_expiry_height_is_below_the_threshold`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L966) |

## [ZIP 204](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0204): Zcash P2P Network Protocol

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A node refuses a message whose magic bytes are not the magic bytes of its network (Mainnet 24e92764, Testnet fa1af9bf, Regtest aae83f5f). | [`DecodeError::Magic`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L400), [`Network::magic`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L83) | [`frame_header_checks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L239) |
| The default ports are 8233 on Mainnet, 18233 on Testnet and 18344 on Regtest. | [`add_listen_port`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/config.rs#L806) | [`the_output_of_zakurad_generate_gives_an_exact_report`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/config.rs#L1542) |
| The ZIP gives the names of the DNS seeders of Mainnet and Testnet. | [`MAINNET_SEEDERS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/connect.rs#L34), [`TESTNET_SEEDERS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/connect.rs#L42) (the seeder lists of Zakura; the list of the ZIP is informational) | [`a_seeder_that_fails_does_not_stop_the_others`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/peers.rs#L613) |
| A node can use a compiled-in list of seed nodes when the DNS seeders fail (option). | Not implemented: the rule is an option, and the DNS seeders and the configured peers give the first addresses. | none |
| A node disconnects a peer that sends nothing within a time limit (recommendation). | [`SessionError::PingTimeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L312), [`ping_timeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L179) | [`ping_keepalive_and_timeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L547) |
| A node closes a connection that sends no `version` message within a time limit (recommendation). | [`handshake_timeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L302), [`handshake_timeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L177) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484) |
| A node closes a connection that sends no `pong` message within a time limit (recommendation). | [`ping_timeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L311), [`ping_timeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L179) | [`ping_keepalive_and_timeout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L547) |
| The command name is printable ASCII up to the first NUL byte and NUL bytes after it, and a node ignores a message that breaks this rule. | [`LegacyMessage::Unknown`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L734) (a command with bad bytes never equals a known command, so the node ignores it) | [`unknown_commands_pass_through`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L329), [`simulated_legacy_peer_with_the_bit_gets_zcmpctver_then_legacy_relay`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/loopback.rs#L517) |
| The payload has at most 2,097,152 bytes, and a node refuses a message with a larger `length`. | [`MAX_BODY_LEN`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L33), [`DecodeError::Oversize`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L407), [`max_body_len`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L291) | [`frame_header_checks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L239) |
| A node verifies the checksum after it reads the payload and refuses a message with a bad checksum. | [`DecodeError::Checksum`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L580), [`DecodeError::Checksum`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L755) | [`frame_header_checks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L239) |
| A CompactSize value has its canonical encoding, and a node refuses a message with a non-canonical CompactSize. | [`Reader::compact_size`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L864), [`zcash_encoding::CompactSize::read`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_encoding/0.4.0/source/src/lib.rs#L33), [`DecodeError::CompactSize`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L877) | [`inv_limits_apply_before_allocation`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L208), [`addrv2_rejects_invalid_lengths`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L438) |
| A `CAddress` has the `time` field outside a `version` message and no `time` field inside it. | [`TimedNetAddr`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L624), [`Reader::net_addr`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L604) | [`zebra_addr_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L123), [`version_frame_matches_the_bitcoin_layout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L38) |
| The service flag `NODE_NETWORK` is bit 0, and bits 24 to 31 are for temporary experiments. | [`NODE_NETWORK`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L85), [`NODE_COMPACT_RELAY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L88) | [`service_bit_is_clear_of_zakura`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L288) |
| A node gets the size of an `inv` or `getdata` message from a parse of each entry (36 or 68 bytes). | [`Reader::inv`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L982), [`MSG_WTX`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L992) | [`zebra_msg_wtx_vector`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L189) |
| A node refuses an inventory entry with an unknown type code. | [`DecodeError::InvType`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L998) (type 0, `MSG_ERROR`, is accepted, as in Zakura; issue hayai-eea) | [`zebra_msg_wtx_vector`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L189) |
| A node refuses `MSG_WTX` when the negotiated protocol version is below 170,014. | [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51), [`min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168) (no peer below 170,014 completes the handshake) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484) |
| A node sends no message other than `version` before it gets the `version` message of the peer. | [`PeerSession::new`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L85), [`LegacyMessage::Ping`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L194) | [`a_ping_before_the_handshake_gets_no_pong`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L530) |

| A node can ask another peer for a block after a stall of 2 s (option). | [`rescue_timeout_ms`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/download.rs#L173), [`rescue_timeout_ms`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/download.rs#L1057) (2 s without progress plus the time of one largest block at the measured rate of the peer) | [`late_bodies_after_a_rescue_are_used_and_give_no_penalty`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/tests/download.rs#L727) |
| A node announces a block with `inv` at once, without the trickle delay. | [`Relay::block_validated`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L2191) (the announcement to a legacy peer waits for the block validation, because Zakura bans the sender of an invalid block) | [`a_legacy_peer_gets_no_block_before_its_validation`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/loopback.rs#L1356) |
| A node announces a transaction of version 4 or earlier with `MSG_TX` and a transaction of version 5 or later with `MSG_WTX`. | [`PRE_V5_AUTH_DIGEST`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L329), [`tx_inv_item`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L2071), [`tx_inv_item`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1532) | [`tx_inv_item_follows_zip239`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L593) |
| A node gives a penalty to a peer that announces a transaction with the wrong inventory type for its version (recommendation). | Not implemented: such a transaction then fails the lookup or the parse, so no verdict changes. | none |
| A node does not send a transaction `inv` at once, and it sends them at random intervals (recommendation). | [`tx_inv_item`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L2071) (the node sends the `inv` at once for a low relay latency; no verdict changes) | none |
| A node does not relay a transaction that expires within 3 blocks of the tip (recommendation). | [`is_relayable`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L322), [`is_relayable`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L574), [`for_each_relay_id`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1532), [`for_each_relay_id`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/mempool.rs#L188) | [`expiry_boundaries`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L584), [`the_answer_to_mempool_leaves_out_a_transaction_that_expires_soon`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/loopback.rs#L583), [`relay_ids_leave_out_a_transaction_that_expires_soon`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L1016) |
| A node stores at most 100 orphan transactions (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai keeps no orphan transactions, so the count is always 0. | none |
| A node does not relay a transaction with a fee below the minimum relay fee (recommendation). | [`PolicyReject::FeeBelowMinimumRelay`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L402), [`min_relay_fee`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L259) | [`minimum_relay_fee_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L983) |
| A node limits the rate of the address records that it takes from a peer, with the token bucket of zcashd (recommendation). | [`AddrBudget::take`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/addrbook.rs#L214), [`addr_budget`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1384) | [`the_budget_of_a_connection_bounds_its_addresses`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/addrbook.rs#L1276) |
| A node keeps a misbehaviour score for each peer (recommendation). | [`ScoreBoard`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/score.rs#L164), [`ScoreBoard::record`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/score.rs#L202), [`PeerManager::record`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/connect.rs#L261) | [`reported_misbehaviour_disconnects_at_the_threshold_and_decays`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/peers.rs#L847) |
| The penalty table gives 1 point for a duplicate `version`, a message before the handshake or a duplicate `verack`, 20 points for the size limits and unconnected headers, and 100 points for Bloom filter faults and the wrong inventory type. | [`Misbehaviour::points`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/score.rs#L64), [`Misbehaviour::Malformed`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1174) (50 points and a disconnect for each decode fault and each message before the handshake; a duplicate `verack` is ignored; issue hayai-b63) | [`a_message_before_the_handshake_disconnects_and_the_second_one_bans`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/peers.rs#L817) |
| In the 1,728 blocks before an activation, a node prefers peers with the new protocol version (recommendation). | Not implemented: before an activation the node does not prefer upgraded peers, and no verdict changes. | none |

## [ZIP 205](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0205): Deployment of the Sapling Network Upgrade

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The Sapling `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` is 0x76b809bb. | [`BranchId::Sapling`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L526), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId::Sapling`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L789) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| The Sapling `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` is 280,000 on Testnet and 419,200 on Mainnet. | [`SpecError::Order`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L305), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Sapling`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L494), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Sapling`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L529) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| A Sapling node advertises at least the protocol version 170,007 on Mainnet and Testnet. | [`PROTOCOL_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L29) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| The minimum peer protocol version stays 170,002. | [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum is 170,150: no peer below it follows the chain after NU6.2, and no block verdict depends on it) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| After the Sapling activation, a node refuses and disconnects the peers without Sapling (recommendation). | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51), [`Relay::set_min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L767) (the minimum 170,150 is above 170,007) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484), [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| On Testnet from the height 299,188, a block more than 15 minutes after its parent has `nBits` = ToCompact(PoWLimit). | [`min_difficulty_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L479), [`min_difficulty_gap_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L490), [`min_difficulty_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L754) | [`testnet_minimum_difficulty_at_the_gap_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L584), [`testnet_minimum_difficulty_blocks_of_the_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L750) |
| The minimum-difficulty change does not apply to Mainnet. | [`min_difficulty_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L708) | [`testnet_minimum_difficulty_at_the_gap_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L584) |

## [ZIP 207](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0207): Funding Streams

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The value of a funding stream is `fs.Value(height) = floor(BlockSubsidy(height) * Numerator / Denominator)`. | [`DENOMINATOR`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L49) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480), [`testnet_streams_have_their_ranges_and_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L513), [`the_last_testnet_streams_follow_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L559), [`schedules_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L268) |
| A funding stream is active when `StartHeight <= height < EndHeight`. | [`set_end`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L326) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480), [`testnet_streams_have_their_ranges_and_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L513), [`the_last_testnet_streams_follow_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L559) |
| Each recipient of a funding stream is a P2SH address or a Sapling address. | [`address_script`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L68) (every recipient in the tables of hayai is a P2SH address) | [`every_address_is_a_p2sh_address_of_its_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L771) |
| Each recipient of a funding stream is a P2SH address, a Sapling address, or `DEFERRED_POOL`. | [`Receiver::Deferred`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L407) | [`the_deferred_stream_has_no_address`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L788), [`every_address_is_a_p2sh_address_of_its_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L771) |
| The recipient of a stream changes every `AddressChangeInterval = PostBlossomHalvingInterval / 48` blocks, through `AddressPeriod`, `AddressIndex` and `Address`. | [`PERIODS_PER_HALVING_INTERVAL`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L53), [`address_period`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L261) | [`the_ecc_address_changes_at_each_period_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L674), [`each_range_has_the_periods_of_the_zakura_address_counts`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L722) |
| From NU7, `AddressPeriod = floor((R(A + I - H1) + height - A) / (R C))`, also for the period of a start height. | [`NU7_SPACING_RATIO`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L273), [`NU7_SPACING_RATIO`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L85) | [`an_address_period_has_three_times_the_blocks_from_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L626), [`the_testnet_funding_streams_across_nu7_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L175) |
| A full node tracks the deferred pool balance `ChainValuePoolBalance^Deferred` as the sum of `totalDeferredOutput`. | [`CoinbaseTerms::deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L355), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L78), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L837) | [`the_deferred_pool_follows_the_terms`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L337), [`the_deferred_pool_of_a_chain_pays_the_disbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L366) |
| Before Canopy, the rule of the founders' reward applies. | [`founders_reward`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L188), [`unmatched_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L369), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (every height with a founders' reward is below the mandatory checkpoint; the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_missing_required_output_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L172), [`coinbases_of_the_block_vectors_pass_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L29) |
| From Canopy, the rule of the founders' reward does not apply, also on Testnet where Canopy is before the first halving. | [`Upgrade::Canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L26) | [`the_reward_is_a_fifth_of_the_subsidy_until_canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L123) |
| From Canopy, the coinbase transaction has at least one output for each active stream, with its value, paid in the prescribed way. | [`require`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L189), [`unmatched_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L369) | [`a_missing_required_output_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L172), [`a_required_output_with_another_amount_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L205), [`a_required_output_with_another_script_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L224), [`coinbases_of_the_block_vectors_pass_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L29) |
| The recipient of a stream is `fs.Recipient(height) = fs.Recipients[fs.RecipientIndex(height)]`. | [`address_period`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L261) | [`the_ecc_address_changes_at_each_period_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L674) |
| The prescribed way to pay a P2SH address is the standard P2SH script. | [`p2sh_script`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L37) | [`address_script_is_the_p2sh_script_of_the_address`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L146) |
| The prescribed way to pay a Sapling address follows ZIP 213, with the zero outgoing viewing key and the lead byte 0x02. | Not implemented: no funding stream and no disbursement has a Sapling recipient. | none |
| From NU6, the output rule applies to each active stream other than `DEFERRED_POOL`. | [`funding_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L199) (a stream without an address adds to the deferred part, not to the outputs) | [`terms_have_the_outputs_of_each_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L78), [`from_nu6_the_value_is_exact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L263) |
| The prescribed way to pay a Sapling or Orchard address follows ZIP 213 with the rules after Heartwood. | Not implemented: no funding stream has a shielded recipient. | none |
| A payment to `DEFERRED_POOL` adds `fs.Value(height)` to the deferred pool. | [`Subsidy::deferred`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L209), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L78) | [`the_deferred_pool_follows_the_terms`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L337) |

## [ZIP 208](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0208): Shorter Block Target Spacing

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `PreBlossomHalvingInterval` is 840,000 and `PreBlossomPoWTargetSpacing` is 150 s. | [`pre_blossom_halving_interval`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L714), [`pre_blossom_halving_interval`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L714), [`PRE_BLOSSOM_TARGET_SPACING`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lib.rs#L69) | [`mainnet_and_testnet_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L1217), [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |
| `PostBlossomPoWTargetSpacing` is 75 s. | [`POST_BLOSSOM_TARGET_SPACING`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lib.rs#L72), [`DifficultyParams::POST_BLOSSOM`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L111) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448), [`timespan_bounds_of_both_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L658) |
| `PoWTargetSpacing(height)` is 150 s before Blossom and 75 s from Blossom. | [`DifficultyParams::POST_BLOSSOM`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L198), [`DifficultyParams::target_spacing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L102) | [`the_target_spacing_changes_at_blossom`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L423) |
| `PostBlossomHalvingInterval` is floor(840,000 · 2) = 1,680,000. | [`NetworkParams::post_blossom_halving_interval`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L272) | [`mainnet_and_testnet_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L1217) |
| `AveragingWindowTimespan`, `MinActualTimespan`, `MaxActualTimespan`, `ActualTimespanDamped`, `ActualTimespanBounded` and `Threshold` take the height as a parameter. | [`averaging_window_timespan`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L540), [`bounded_timespan`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L584), [`DifficultyParams::target_spacing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L102) | [`timespan_bounds_of_both_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L658), [`the_target_spacing_changes_at_blossom`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L423) |
| `Halving(height)` has the Blossom case. | [`halving`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L22), [`POST_BLOSSOM_TARGET_SPACING`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L48) | [`mainnet_subsidy_follows_the_schedule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L69), [`testnet_subsidy_follows_the_schedule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L90) |
| `BlockSubsidy(height)` has the Blossom case. | [`block_subsidy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L67), [`MAX_BLOCK_SUBSIDY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L37) | [`mainnet_subsidy_follows_the_schedule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L69), [`testnet_subsidy_follows_the_schedule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L90) |
| `FounderAddressAdjustedHeight` replaces the height in `FounderAddressIndex`. | [`Upgrade::Blossom`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L71), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (every height with a founders' reward is below the mandatory checkpoint; the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`the_address_changes_every_17709_adjusted_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L193) |
| `FoundersRewardLastBlockHeight` is the last height with `Halving < 1`, and no block after it or at the height 0 pays a founders' reward. | [`subsidy::halving`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L49), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`the_reward_is_a_fifth_of_the_subsidy_until_canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L123) |
| `PoWAveragingWindow` and `PoWMedianBlockSpan` do not change at Blossom. | [`DifficultyParams::POST_BLOSSOM`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L111), [`MEDIAN_TIME_SPAN`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lib.rs#L78) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |
| On Testnet from the height 299,188, a block more than `6 · PoWTargetSpacing(height)` after its parent has `nBits` = ToCompact(PoWLimit). | [`min_difficulty_gap_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L490), [`min_difficulty_gap_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L94) | [`testnet_minimum_difficulty_at_the_gap_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L584) |
| From NU7 on Testnet, the gap of the minimum-difficulty rule is 18 target spacings (ZIP 218). | [`DifficultyParams::POST_NU7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L118) | [`the_window_and_the_spacing_change_at_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L453) |
| The halt interval of the End-of-Service follows Blossom (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai has no End-of-Service halt. | none |
| The default expiry delta is 40 blocks after Blossom (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai makes no transaction except the coinbase transaction, whose expiry is its height from NU5. | none |
| A set `-txexpirydelta` applies before and after Blossom (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai makes no transaction except the coinbase transaction and has no such setting. | none |
| A mitigation against fingerprints uses the target spacing of the height of the work estimate (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai has no such mitigation. | none |

## [ZIP 209](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0209): Prohibit Out-of-Range Chain Value Pool Balances

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| No chain value pool (transparent, Sprout, Sapling, Orchard, Ironwood, deferred) is negative after a block. | [`ContextError::NegativeValuePool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L822), [`ContextError::NegativeTransparentPool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L828), [`CoinbaseTerms::deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L837), [`CoinbaseTerms::deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L355) | [`no_value_pool_can_be_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1413), [`the_deferred_pool_pays_the_disbursement_or_the_block_fails`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1493), [`the_sprout_pool_is_never_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L615), [`the_ironwood_pool_does_not_go_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L677) |
| The total of the chain value pools, computed without overflow, is at most `MAX_MONEY`. | [`checked_sum`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L839), [`MAX_MONEY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L238), [`MAX_MONEY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L817) | [`the_total_of_the_pools_is_bounded`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1617) |
| The Sprout pool balance is the sum of `vpub_old` minus the sum of `vpub_new`. | [`net_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L748), [`sprout_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L726) | [`each_value_pool_follows_its_change`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1379), [`the_sprout_pool_is_never_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L615) |
| The Sapling, Orchard and Ironwood pool balances are the negated sum of the value balances of their pool. | [`value_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L729), [`value_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L732), [`value_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L735), [`ContextError::NegativeValuePool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L822) | [`each_value_pool_follows_its_change`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1379), [`the_value_pools_follow_the_block`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L700) |
| The Orchard pool balance is zero before NU5, and the Ironwood pool balance is zero before NU6.3. | [`ContextError::PoolNotActive`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L679), [`PrepareError::PoolNotActive`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L245) (no bundle of a pool is valid before its upgrade, so its balance stays zero) | none |
| A node can relay a transaction that cannot be mined because of this rule (option). | Not implemented: the rule is an option of the relay policy and changes no block verdict. | none |
| Nodes enforce the rule from acceptance at every height, without a network upgrade (recommendation). | [`value_pools_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L863), [`block_pools_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check/checkpoint.rs#L125) (the full path and the checkpoint path apply the rule at every height) | [`the_checkpoint_path_keeps_the_checks_that_guard_the_state`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/checkpoint.rs#L269) |

## [ZIP 211](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0211): Disabling Addition of New Value to the Sprout Chain Value Pool

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|

## [ZIP 212](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0212): Allow Recipient to Derive Ephemeral Secret from Note Plaintext

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A sender uses the lead byte 0x02 and derives `rcm` and `esk` from `rseed`. | Not implemented: the rule binds a wallet that sends notes; hayai has no wallet. | none |
| A receiver accepts the lead byte 0x01 or 0x02 in a grace period of 32,256 blocks, then only 0x02. | Not implemented: the rule binds a wallet that receives notes; a node applies only the coinbase rule of this ZIP. | none |
| For a note with the lead byte 0x02, the receiver checks that `epk = [esk] g_d`. | [`try_output_recovery_with_ovk`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L42), [`zcash_note_encryption::ka_derive_public`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_note_encryption/0.4.2/source/src/lib.rs#L563) (coinbase outputs only) | [`a_sapling_coinbase_output_decrypts_only_with_the_zero_key`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L261) |
| From Canopy, a Sapling coinbase output that decrypts by ZIP 213 has the lead byte 0x02, also in the grace period. | [`Zip212Enforcement::On`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L98), [`sapling_crypto::plaintext_version_is_valid`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/note_encryption.rs#L82) | [`the_sapling_lead_byte_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L275) |
| An Orchard coinbase output always has the lead byte 0x02. | [`OrchardDomain::for_action`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L55), [`orchard::NOTE_VERSION`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/note_encryption.rs#L98) | none (issue hayai-8od) |

## [ZIP 213](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0213): Shielded Coinbase

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A coinbase transaction has no JoinSplit and no Spend description. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseJoinSplit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L434), [`PrepareError::CoinbaseShieldedSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L439) | [`a_coinbase_has_no_joinsplit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1867) (Sapling spend: none, issue hayai-cvi) |
| Before Heartwood, a coinbase transaction has no Output description. | [`CoinbaseError::ShieldedOutputBeforeHeartwood`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L93) (on Mainnet and Testnet the checkpoint list also covers these heights) | [`a_coinbase_has_no_shielded_output_before_heartwood`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L311) |
| The `valueBalance`, Output and `bindingSig` rules of other transactions apply to a coinbase transaction. | [`ScopedBatch::add`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L216), [`BatchValidator::check_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L91) (the same verification path as every other transaction) | [`the_coinbase_value_counts_the_ironwood_output`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L766) |
| The coinbase spend rules apply only to transparent coinbase outputs. | [`is_coinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L497) | [`mature_coinbase_spent_to_shielded_outputs_is_accepted`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L301) |
| Every shielded coinbase output decrypts with the all-zero outgoing viewing key and gives a valid note commitment. | [`sapling_output_decrypts`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L102), [`orchard_action_decrypts`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L107), [`ironwood_action_decrypts`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L112), [`zcash_note_encryption::try_output_recovery_with_ovk`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_note_encryption/0.4.2/source/src/lib.rs#L636) | [`a_sapling_coinbase_output_decrypts_only_with_the_zero_key`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L261), [`an_orchard_coinbase_output_decrypts_only_with_the_zero_key`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L333), [`an_ironwood_coinbase_output_decrypts_only_with_the_zero_key`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L347), [`draft_checks_the_shielded_outputs_of_a_coinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L361) |
| From NU6.3, a coinbase transaction has no Orchard-pool component. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseOrchardBundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L444) | [`the_coinbase_rules_of_the_orchard_and_ironwood_bundles`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1528) |

## [ZIP 215](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0215): Explicitly Defining and Modifying Ed25519 Validation Rules

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The Ed25519 values `A` and `R` are encodings of points, and a non-canonical encoding is accepted. | [`VerificationKey::try_from`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L166), [`ed25519_zebra::decompress`](https://docs.rs/crate/ed25519-zebra/4.2.0/source/src/verification_key.rs#L164), [`ed25519_zebra::decompress`](https://docs.rs/crate/ed25519-zebra/4.2.0/source/src/verification_key.rs#L267) | [`the_embedded_key_verifies_the_published_joinsplits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L83) (edge cases: issue hayai-8od) |
| The Ed25519 value `S` is less than ℓ. | [`VerificationKey::verify`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L170), [`ed25519_zebra::from_canonical_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/ed25519-zebra/4.2.0/source/src/verification_key.rs#L264) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| Ed25519 verification uses the cofactored equation [8][S]B = [8]R + [8][k]A, not the cofactorless equation. | [`VerificationKey::verify`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L170), [`ed25519_zebra::mul_by_cofactor`](https://docs.rs/crate/ed25519-zebra/4.2.0/source/src/verification_key.rs#L278) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| The Ed25519 rules of ZIP 215 apply from Canopy. | [`VerificationKey::verify`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L170), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326) (the rules apply at every height; no block before Canopy gets full validation, because the checkpoint list covers these heights) | none |

## [ZIP 216](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0216): Require Canonical Jubjub Point Encodings

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A non-canonical encoding of `R` in `spendAuthSig` is invalid. | [`BatchValidator::check_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L91), [`sapling_crypto::queue`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier/batch.rs#L75), [`reddsa::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/reddsa/0.5.2/source/src/batch.rs#L224) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| A non-canonical encoding of `R` in `bindingSigSapling` is invalid. | [`BatchValidator::check_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L91), [`sapling_crypto::queue`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier/batch.rs#L120), [`reddsa::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/reddsa/0.5.2/source/src/batch.rs#L224) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| A non-canonical encoding of `pk*_d`, or the zero point, in the plaintext of `C^out` is invalid (coinbase outputs). | [`try_output_recovery_with_ovk`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L42), [`sapling_crypto::DiversifiedTransmissionKey::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/note_encryption.rs#L268), [`sapling_crypto::is_identity`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/address.rs#L50) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| The encodings of `cv`, `rk` and `epk` are canonical. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::from_bytes_not_small_order`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L148), [`zcash_primitives::VerificationKey::try_from`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L203), [`sapling_crypto::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier/batch.rs#L90) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
