| The NU7 constants are `GlobalShieldedBudget` 330, `OrchardProtocolBlockActionLimit` 330, `SaplingBlockIOLimit` 300 and `SproutBlockJoinSplitLimit` 0. | [`BlockLimits::NU7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/block_limits.rs#L34) | [`the_nu7_rule_set_changes_these_rules`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L395), [`the_limits_and_the_difficulty_parameters_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L390) |
| From NU7, the Orchard actions of a block are at most 330. | [`ContextError::TooManyOrchardActions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L700) | [`block_totals`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L573) |
| From NU7, the Ironwood actions of a block are at most 330. | [`ContextError::TooManyIronwoodActions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L704) | [`ironwood_actions_count_for_the_block_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L712) |
| From NU7, the Sapling spends and outputs of a block are at most 300. | [`ContextError::TooManySaplingIos`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L710) | none |
| From NU7, the JoinSplits of a block are at most 0. | [`ShieldedPools::sprout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L306), [`ContextError::PoolNotActive`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L679), [`PrepareError::PoolNotActive`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L245) (no count: the pool rule refuses each transaction with a JoinSplit) | [`the_nu7_rule_set_changes_these_rules`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L395), [`a_v4_transaction_is_refused_from_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L905) |
| From NU7, the Orchard actions plus the Ironwood actions plus the Sapling spends and outputs plus 2 for each JoinSplit of a block are at most 330. | [`ContextError::ShieldedCostAboveBudget`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L717) (the JoinSplit term is 0, because the pool rule refuses each JoinSplit) | [`ironwood_actions_count_for_the_block_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L712) |
| The limits do not apply to the transparent parts, and the block size limit of 2 MB stays. | [`MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L289) | [`oversized_block_is_rejected_before_parsing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L620) |
| `PoWMedianBlockSpan` does not change at NU7. | [`MEDIAN_TIME_SPAN`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lib.rs#L78) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |
| On Testnet from NU7, a block more than 18 · 25 s = 450 s after its parent has `nBits` = ToCompact(PoWLimit); 451 s qualifies and 450 s does not. | [`min_difficulty_gap_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L490), [`min_difficulty_gap_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L121) | [`the_window_and_the_spacing_change_at_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L453) |
| The default expiry delta becomes 120 blocks after NU7 (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai makes no transaction other than the coinbase, so it has no default expiry delta. | none |
| A set `-txexpirydelta` applies before and after NU7 (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai makes no transaction other than the coinbase, so it has no expiry delta setting. | none |
| `COINBASE_MATURITY` stays 100 (recommendation). | [`COINBASE_MATURITY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lib.rs#L61), [`COINBASE_MATURITY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L501) | [`immature_coinbase_spend_is_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L263) |
| `MAX_REORG_LENGTH` becomes 600 (recommendation). | [`FINALITY_DEPTH`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/lib.rs#L75), [`finality_depth`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L437) (1,000 blocks at every height; the value is not a block rule) | [`branches_below_the_finalized_height_are_removed_and_refused`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/tests/headers.rs#L518) |
| `TX_EXPIRING_SOON_THRESHOLD` stays 3 (recommendation). | [`TX_EXPIRING_SOON_THRESHOLD`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L31) | [`a_real_transaction_meets_the_context_rules_at_their_boundaries`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/mempool.rs#L66) |
| `MAX_BLOCKS_IN_TRANSIT_PER_PEER` becomes 48 (recommendation). | [`peer_in_flight_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/download.rs#L169) (64 blocks and 8 MB for each peer at every height; no block verdict depends on the value) | none |
| `BLOCK_DOWNLOAD_WINDOW` becomes 3,072 (recommendation). | [`window_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/download.rs#L164) (1,024 blocks and a memory budget of 1 GiB at every height; no block verdict depends on the value) | none |
| `MIN_BLOCKS_TO_KEEP` becomes 864 (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai does not prune blocks. | none |
| `NETWORK_UPGRADE_PEER_PREFERENCE_BLOCK_PERIOD` stays 1,728 (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai does not prefer upgraded peers before an activation; the value changes only the peer selection, not a block verdict. | none |
| The recommended anchor depth stays 3 blocks (recommendation). | Not implemented: the rule binds a wallet; hayai has no wallet. | none |

## [ZIP 221](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0221): FlyClient - Consensus-Layer Changes

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The header of a block commits to the root of the Merkle mountain range of the blocks from the last upgrade activation to the parent. | [`ContextError::BlockCommitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L961), [`header_commitment`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L155) (a shadow node that does not know the history tree does not check it: issue hayai-k6y) | [`mainnet_canopy_activation_resets_the_tree`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L277), [`the_header_commits_to_the_history_tree_of_version_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L316), [`tampered_blocks_fail_at_the_right_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/validate.rs#L83) |
| The tree holds the blocks from the last upgrade activation, and each activation starts a new tree. | [`HistoryState::append`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L384), [`history_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L411) | [`appends_check_heights_and_start_a_new_tree_per_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L359), [`mainnet_canopy_activation_resets_the_tree`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L277) |
| A node hash is BLAKE2b-256 with the personalization `ZcashHistory` and the branch id of the epoch of the block. | [`consensus_branch_id`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L209), [`zcash_history::personalization`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_history/0.5.0/source/src/version.rs#L21) | [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94), [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125) |
| Field `hashSubtreeCommitment` is the block hash in a leaf and the hash of the two serialized children in an inner node. | [`subtree_commitment`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L210), [`zcash_history::blake2b_personal`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_history/0.5.0/source/src/version.rs#L59) | [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94), [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125), [`mainnet_heartwood_activation`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L219) |
| Fields `nEarliestTimestamp` and `nLatestTimestamp` are the header time in a leaf, and an inner node takes them from its left and right child. | [`start_time`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L211), [`zcash_history::start_time`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_history/0.5.0/source/src/node_data.rs#L71) | [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94), [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125) |
| Fields `nEarliestTargetBits` and `nLatestTargetBits` are the `nBits` of the header in a leaf, and an inner node takes them from its left and right child. | [`start_target`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L213), [`zcash_history::start_target`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_history/0.5.0/source/src/node_data.rs#L73) | [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94), [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125) |
| Fields `hashEarliestSaplingRoot` and `hashLatestSaplingRoot` are the final Sapling root of the block in a leaf. | [`start_sapling_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L215), [`HistoryLeaf::from_block`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L92) | [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94), [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125), [`mainnet_heartwood_activation`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L219) |
| Field `nSubTreeTotalWork` is floor(2^256 / (ToTarget(nBits) + 1)) in a leaf and the sum of the children modulo 2^256 in an inner node. | [`block_work`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L130), [`block_work`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/difficulty.rs#L44), [`zcash_history::subtree_total_work`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_history/0.5.0/source/src/node_data.rs#L77) | [`work_of_known_targets`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L548), [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94), [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125) |
| Fields `nEarliestHeight` and `nLatestHeight` are the block height in a leaf, and the heights of the leaves follow each other. | [`start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L218), [`HistoryError::Height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L391) | [`appends_check_heights_and_start_a_new_tree_per_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L359), [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63) |
| Field `nSaplingTxCount` is the number of transactions with Sapling spends or outputs. | [`sapling_tx`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L100) | none |
| From NU5, fields `hashEarliestOrchardRoot` and `hashLatestOrchardRoot` are the final Orchard root of the block in a leaf. | [`start_orchard_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L234) | [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94) |
| From NU5, field `nOrchardTxCount` is the number of transactions with Orchard actions. | [`orchard_tx`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L107), [`orchard_tx`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L236) | none |
| A node before NU5 has no NU5 fields, so its size is 147 to 171 bytes, and from NU5 its size is 212 to 244 bytes. | [`TreeVersion::V1`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L194), [`TreeVersion::V2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L195), [`HistoryVersion::V1`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L205), [`HistoryVersion::V2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L229) | [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94) |
| `hashChainHistoryRoot` is the BLAKE2b-256 hash of the serialized root node, after the peaks are bagged from left to right. | [`V::hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L476), [`zcash_history::hash`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_history/0.5.0/source/src/version.rs#L102) | [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63), [`zip_0221_v2_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L94), [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125) |
| An append merges the peaks from right to left and then bags them. | [`append_leaf`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L465), [`zcash_history::Tree::append_leaf`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_history/0.5.0/source/src/tree.rs#L142), [`peak_positions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L492) | [`peak_positions_match_the_binary_decomposition`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L536), [`zip_0221_v1_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L63) |
| A reorganization deletes the rightmost leaves of the tree. | [`history`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/lib.rs#L180), [`Chain::pop`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/lib.rs#L664) (no delete: each layer keeps its own tree, so a pop gives the tree of the parent again) | [`pop_restores_the_previous_view`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/tests.rs#L211) |
| From Sapling until Heartwood, `hashLightClientRoot` is the final Sapling root of the block. | [`BranchId::Sapling`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L163), [`ContextError::BlockCommitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L961) | [`mainnet_heartwood_activation`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L219) |
| The header field of the Heartwood activation block is 32 zero bytes, and it is not a root hash. | [`HistoryState::empty`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L276), [`root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L168) | [`mainnet_heartwood_activation`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L219) |
| In a later Heartwood or Canopy block, the header field is the `hashChainHistoryRoot` of the tree of the parent, and from NU5 this root goes into `hashBlockCommitments` (ZIP 244). | [`BranchId::Heartwood`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L167), [`block_commitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L170) | [`mainnet_canopy_activation_resets_the_tree`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L277), [`nu5_and_later_commit_to_the_root_and_the_auth_data_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L323) |
| The byte format and the version of the block header do not change. | Not implemented: the rule changes no header field, so no check applies to it. | none |
| The tree starts at Heartwood on Mainnet and Testnet, and the Orchard fields start at NU5. | [`HistoryVersion::V1`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L205), [`HistoryVersion::V2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L229) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |
| From NU6.3, fields `hashEarliestIronwoodRoot` and `hashLatestIronwoodRoot` are the final Ironwood root of the block in a leaf (ZIP 258). | [`start_ironwood_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L251) | [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125) |
| From NU6.3, field `nIronwoodTxCount` is the number of transactions with Ironwood actions (ZIP 258). | [`ironwood_tx`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L108), [`ironwood_tx`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L253) | [`zip_0221_v3_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L125) |
| A node before NU6.3 has no NU6.3 fields, and from NU6.3 its size is 277 to 317 bytes (ZIP 258). | [`TreeVersion::V3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L196), [`HistoryVersion::V3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L283) | [`appends_check_heights_and_start_a_new_tree_per_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L359) |

## [ZIP 225](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0225): Version 5 Transaction Format

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A v5 transaction has the header, the branch id, the lock time, the expiry height, and the transparent, Sapling and Orchard components, in this order. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L137), [`zcash_primitives::read_v5`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L850), [`scan_v5_v6`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L214) | [`upstream_transaction_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L111), [`mainnet_block_transactions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L84) |
| `valueBalanceSapling` and `bindingSigSapling` are present only when the transaction has Sapling spends or outputs, and `anchorSapling` is present only when it has Sapling spends. | [`zcash_primitives::read_v5_bundle`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L450), [`sapling_v5`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L342) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| The Orchard fields are present only when `nActionsOrchard` is above 0. | [`zcash_primitives::read_bundle`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L104), [`orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L370) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| The Sapling proofs and signatures and the Orchard signatures are in the order of their descriptions. | [`zcash_primitives::read_zkproof`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L469), [`zcash_primitives::read_signature`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L127) | none |
| A coinbase transaction has `enableSpendsOrchard` set to 0. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseShieldedSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L439) | [`the_coinbase_rules_of_the_orchard_and_ironwood_bundles`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1528) |

## [ZIP 229](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0229): Version 6 Transaction Format

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A v6 transaction has `fOverwintered` set, the version 6 and the version group 0xD884B698. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L137), [`zcash_primitives::V6_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L92), [`V6_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L121) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| Bits 3 to 7 of `flagsOrchard` are 0, and bit 2 is `enableCrossAddress`. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L137), [`orchard::FLAGS_ALWAYS_EXPECTED_UNSET`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle.rs#L403) | [`the_flag_bits_of_each_pool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1386) |
| Bits 3 to 7 of `flagsIronwood` are 0. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L137), [`orchard::FLAGS_ALWAYS_EXPECTED_UNSET`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle.rs#L403) | [`the_flag_bits_of_each_pool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1386) |
| `sizeProofs` of each Orchard-protocol pool is 2720 + 2272 × the number of actions. | [`zcash_primitives::try_from_parts`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L142), [`orchard::validate_proof_size`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle.rs#L951), [`orchard::Proof::expected_proof_size`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/lib.rs#L111) | [`a_proof_has_the_canonical_length`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1456) |
| From NU6.3, the transaction version is 4, 5 or 6. | [`tx_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198), [`tx_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L278) | [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682) |
| A v6 transaction has the version group 0xD884B698. | [`zcash_primitives::V6_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L92), [`V6_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L121) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| `nActionsIronwood` is below 2^16. | [`MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L289) (a block of at most 2,000,000 bytes cannot hold 2^16 actions) | [`oversized_block_is_rejected_before_parsing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L620) |
| A v5 transaction has a source of funds: a transparent input, a Sapling spend, or Orchard actions with `enableSpendsOrchard` set. | [`PrepareError::NoSource`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L389) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292) |
| A v6 transaction has a source of funds, and Ironwood actions with `enableSpendsIronwood` set are also a source. | [`PrepareError::NoSource`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L389) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292) |
| A v5 or v6 transaction has a sink of funds under the same rule. | [`PrepareError::NoSink`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L399) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292) |
| Ironwood actions have `enableSpendsIronwood` or `enableOutputsIronwood` set. | [`PrepareError::IronwoodFlags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L414) | [`actions_need_an_enable_flag`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1355) |
| `bindingSigIronwood` is a valid signature. | [`add_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L103), [`orchard::binding_validating_key`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L99) | [`bisection_isolates_the_tampered_orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L162), [`invalid_proofs_and_keys_fail_the_shielded_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L381) |
| From NU6.3, a coinbase transaction has no Orchard-pool actions. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseOrchardBundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L444), [`orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L285) | [`the_coinbase_rules_of_the_orchard_and_ironwood_bundles`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1528) |
| A v5 or v6 coinbase transaction has `enableSpendsOrchard` set to 0. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseShieldedSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L439) | [`the_coinbase_rules_of_the_orchard_and_ironwood_bundles`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1528) |
| A v6 coinbase transaction has `enableSpendsIronwood` set to 0. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseShieldedSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L439) | [`the_coinbase_rules_of_the_orchard_and_ironwood_bundles`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1528), [`context_free_rules_reject_at_the_block_level`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L462) |
| In a v5 or v6 transaction, the reserved bits 2 to 7 of `flagsOrchard` are 0. | [`orchard::ValuePool::Orchard`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle.rs#L414), [`PrepareError::OrchardCrossAddress`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L421) | [`the_flag_bits_of_each_pool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1386) |
| The proofs and the signatures of each pool are in the order of its actions. | [`zcash_primitives::read_signature`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L127) | none |
| The v6 txid is the ZIP 244 digest tree with `ironwood_digest_v6`, and the anchors are not in the effecting data. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L137), [`zcash_primitives::to_hash_v6`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/txid.rs#L423) | none (no published v6 txid vector) |
| The v6 authorizing digest has the Ironwood digest last, the anchors in the pool digests, and the v6 personalizations. | [`IRONWOOD_SIGS_V6`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L484), [`auth_digest`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L436) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| The v6 signature hash is the ZIP 244 tree with the v6 digests. | [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::v6_signature_hash`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash.rs#L53) | [`a_nu6_3_block_validates_cold_and_warm_to_identical_layers`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L159) |
| `hashAuthDataRoot` uses the v6 authorizing digests. | [`auth_data_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L388) | [`the_header_commits_to_the_history_tree_of_version_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L316) |

## [ZIP 235](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0235): Remove 60% of Transaction Fees From Circulation

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `NSMFeeContribution` is floor(6 × `TransactionFees` / 10), computed on the total fees of the block. | [`miner_fee_share`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L48), [`fees`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L720) | [`the_miner_gets_the_fees_minus_six_tenths_rounded_down`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L218), [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| `MinerFees` is `TransactionFees` − `NSMFeeContribution`. | [`miner_fee_share`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L48), [`CoinbaseTerms::miner_fees`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L253) | [`the_miner_gets_the_fees_minus_six_tenths_rounded_down`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L218), [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| From NU7, the total input value of the coinbase is `BlockSubsidy` + `MinerFees` + `totalDeferredInput`. | [`CoinbaseTerms::payable`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L267), [`nsm_fee_share`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L311) | [`the_terms_at_the_nu7_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L387), [`the_coinbase_follows_the_rules_at_the_nu7_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/coinbase.rs#L388), [`the_coinbase_terms_across_nu7_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L221) |
| The exact value rule of ZIP 236 applies to this input value. | [`CoinbaseError::ValueNotExact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L337) | [`the_terms_at_the_nu7_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L387) |
| The fee contribution goes into no chain value pool and is not part of `IssuedSupply`. | [`value_pools_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L804) (the fees leave the pools, and the coinbase adds only `MinerFees`) | none |
| With ZIP 237, the fee contribution is part of `removed(height)` and goes to the NSM value balance. | [`balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L25) (the closed form counts the contribution through the chain value pools, with the same value) | [`the_balance_is_the_scheduled_issuance_minus_the_pools`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L76), [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| The change applies to Mainnet and Testnet from NU7. | [`nsm_fee_share`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L311) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |

## [ZIP 236](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0236): Blocks should balance exactly

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| From NU6, the total output value of the coinbase is equal to its total input value. | [`CoinbaseError::ValueNotExact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L337), [`exact_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L242) | [`from_nu6_the_value_is_exact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L263), [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448), [`a_changed_coinbase_of_the_consensus_rule_fails_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/coinbase.rs#L316) |
| Before NU6, the total output value of the coinbase is at most its total input value. | [`CoinbaseError::ValueAboveLimit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L344) | [`before_nu6_the_value_is_a_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L298) |
| The change applies to Mainnet and Testnet. | [`exact_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L242) | [`terms_have_the_outputs_of_each_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L78) |

## [ZIP 237](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0237): Network Sustainability Mechanism: Halving-Preserving Issuance

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `LN2_SCALED` is 6,931,680,000. | [`REISSUANCE_NUMERATOR`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L34) (hayai stores the derived numerator 1,375, and the test derives it from `LN2_SCALED`) | [`the_reissuance_fraction_is_the_one_of_zip_237`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L57) |
| `BLOCK_SUBSIDY_FRACTION(height)` is floor(`LN2_SCALED` / `HalvingInterval(height)`) / 10^10, which is 1,375 / 10^10 from NU7. | [`REISSUANCE_NUMERATOR`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L34), [`REISSUANCE_DENOMINATOR`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L35) (a constant: the reissuance height is at or above NU7 on Mainnet and Testnet, so the value is the same) | [`the_reissuance_fraction_is_the_one_of_zip_237`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L57) |
| `INITIAL_NSM_VALUE_BALANCE` is `S(H)` − `IssuedSupply(H)` at the height before NU7. | [`expected_seed`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L63), [`ConsensusError::NsmSeedMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L118) (the measured value of each network and an equality check at the height before NU7; the ZIP gives an estimate only) | [`the_balance_rules_start_at_the_block_before_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L96), [`the_nsm_rules_apply_from_the_block_before_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1528) |
| `DEPLOYMENT_BLOCK_HEIGHT` is the first height `h` in [max(A, H3 + 1), H4) where ceil(1375 × (`MAX_MONEY` − S_A(h − 1)) / 10^10) is below B_A(h). | [`reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L38) | [`the_reissuance_height_of_each_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L141), [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| The closed form h0 + ceil(max(0, R0 − Rmax) / b) gives this height. | [`reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L38) | [`the_reissuance_height_of_each_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L141), [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| The NU7 activation heights are such that the deployment height exists before H4. | Not implemented: the rule applies to the choice of the NU7 height, not to a node, and the Testnet height satisfies it. | none |
| The reissuance height is 16,235,274 − 2A on Testnet (7,305,222 with the NU7 height of ZIP 259) and 16,026,474 − 2A on Mainnet. | [`reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L38) | [`the_reissuance_height_of_each_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L141) |
| `ScheduledBlockSubsidy` is the `BlockSubsidy` of specification §7.8 with the ZIP 218 changes. | [`block_subsidy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L67) | [`the_testnet_schedule_follows_the_nu7_spacing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L164), [`the_schedule_across_nu7_matches_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L130) |
| `AdditionalBlockSubsidy(height)` is 0 below the deployment height, and ceil(`BLOCK_SUBSIDY_FRACTION` × `NSMValueBalance(height − 1)`) from it. | [`nsm::reissuance_active`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L163), [`reissuance_bonus`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L195) | [`the_subsidy_has_the_reissuance_bonus_from_the_reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L433), [`the_bonus_rounds_up`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L246), [`the_subsidy_with_the_reissuance_bonus_matches_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L348) |
| `BlockSubsidy` is `ScheduledBlockSubsidy` + `AdditionalBlockSubsidy`. | [`nsm::reissuance_bonus`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L170) | [`the_subsidy_has_the_reissuance_bonus_from_the_reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L433), [`the_coinbase_has_the_reissuance_bonus_from_the_reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/coinbase.rs#L453) |
| `FoundersReward`, `fs.Value`, `totalDeferredOutput`, `MinerSubsidy` and the coinbase input value use the new `BlockSubsidy`. | [`funding::funding_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L199), [`CoinbaseTerms::miner_subsidy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L179) | none (on Testnet the funding streams end at 4,497,948, before the reissuance height 7,305,222) |
| `NSMValueBalance` is 0 below the height before NU7, the initial value at that height, and then the previous value − `AdditionalBlockSubsidy` + `removed`. | [`balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L25) (the closed form `S(height)` − `IssuedSupply(height)`, which is equal to the recursion because each block changes the pools by `Scheduled` + `Additional` − `removed`) | [`the_balance_is_the_scheduled_issuance_minus_the_pools`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L76), [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| `removed(height)` is the value that a deployed mechanism removes, which in NU7 is only the ZIP 235 contribution. | [`balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L25) (the contribution leaves the chain value pools, so the closed form counts it) | [`the_balance_is_the_scheduled_issuance_minus_the_pools`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L76), [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| The NSM value balance is consensus state, but it is not a chain value pool and not part of `IssuedSupply`. | [`balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L25) (hayai derives the balance from the pools and the schedule and stores no value) | [`the_balance_is_the_scheduled_issuance_minus_the_pools`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L76) |
| From NU7, a block that makes `NSMValueBalance` negative is not valid. | [`ConsensusError::NegativeNsmBalance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lib.rs#L112), [`nsm::check_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L865) | [`the_balance_rules_start_at_the_block_before_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L96), [`the_nsm_rules_apply_from_the_block_before_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1528) |
| The changes apply to Mainnet and Testnet. | [`expected_seed`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/nsm.rs#L63), [`reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L38) | [`the_reissuance_height_of_each_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L141) |
| ZIP 237 is not deployed together with ZIP 234. | Not implemented: hayai has no ZIP 234 code, and NU7 does not deploy ZIP 234. | none |

## [ZIP 239](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0239): Relay of Version 5 Transactions

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The `MSG_WTX` inventory type (5) in `inv` and `getdata` carries the 64 bytes of `txid` and `auth_digest`. | [`MSG_WTX`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L108), [`MSG_WTX`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L992), [`MSG_WTX`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L516) | [`zebra_msg_wtx_vector`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L189) |
| A node announces a v5 transaction with `MSG_WTX`. | [`tx_inv_item`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L328) | [`tx_inv_item_follows_zip239`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L593) |
| A transaction that a node sends for a `getdata` `MSG_WTX` request has the encoding of the specification. | [`InvItem::Wtx`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1500) (the node sends the stored wire bytes) | [`simulated_legacy_peer_with_the_bit_gets_zcmpctver_then_legacy_relay`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/loopback.rs#L517) |
| A node does not use `MSG_WTX` for transactions of version 4 and earlier. | [`PRE_V5_AUTH_DIGEST`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L329) | [`tx_inv_item_follows_zip239`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L593) |
| A node does not use `MSG_WTX` on a connection below the protocol version 170,014. | [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51), [`min_peer_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168) (no such connection completes the handshake) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484) |
| The deployment version is 170,014 on Testnet and Mainnet. | [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version 170,150 is above 170,014) | none |
| Before NU5, a node does not advertise, fetch or provide v5 transactions. | [`InvItem::Wtx`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1426), [`RuleSet::tx_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198) (the store holds no v5 transaction before NU5, so the node announces and serves none) | [`before_nu5_a_wtxid_announcement_is_not_fetched`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/loopback.rs#L1471) |
| A node parses the whole message and refuses it when an entry has an unknown type (recommendation). | [`DecodeError::InvType`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L998) (type 0 is accepted; issue hayai-eea) | [`zebra_msg_wtx_vector`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L189) |
| The `getdata` types are `MSG_TX`, `MSG_BLOCK` and `MSG_FILTERED_BLOCK` with 36 bytes, and `MSG_WTX` with 68 bytes. | [`MSG_FILTERED_BLOCK`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L991), [`MSG_WTX`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/codec.rs#L992), [`InvItem::FilteredBlock`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1507) (a `MSG_FILTERED_BLOCK` request gets `notfound`) | [`zebra_msg_wtx_vector`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/tests/codec.rs#L189) |
| A node does not send `MSG_FILTERED_BLOCK` in an `inv` message (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai never sends a `MSG_FILTERED_BLOCK` entry, so the rule always holds. | none |
| A node ignores a `MSG_FILTERED_BLOCK` entry of an `inv` message (recommendation). | [`InvItem::FilteredBlock`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/policy.rs#L1446) | none |

## [ZIP 243](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0243): Transaction Signature Validation for Sapling

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| v4 signatures use the ZIP 243 signature hash. | [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L125), [`zcash_primitives::v4_signature_hash`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash.rs#L48) | [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593), [`every_fixture_transaction_prepares`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L39) (the ZIP 243 vectors do not run against `SighashContext`: issue hayai-xya) |
| A v4 signature hash uses the raw byte of the hash type. | [`SighashType::from_raw`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L151) | [`a_flipped_signature_byte_is_a_script_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L86) |
| The signature hash commits to the consensus branch id. | [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::consensus_branch_id`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash_v4.rs#L142) | [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |

## [ZIP 244](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0244): Transaction Identifier Non-Malleability

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The v5 txid is the T.1 to T.4 digest tree with the personalization `ZcashTxHash_` and the branch id. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L137), [`zcash_primitives::to_txid`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/txid.rs#L475), [`zcash_primitives::ZCASH_TX_PERSONALIZATION_PREFIX`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/txid.rs#L395) | [`upstream_transaction_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L111), [ZIP 244 vectors](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/vectors/tx-zip0244.hex) |
| The v5 signature hash is the S.1 to S.4 digest tree. | [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::v5_signature_hash`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash.rs#L51) | [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) (the ZIP 244 vectors do not run against `SighashContext`: issue hayai-xya) |
| From v5, the only hash types are 0x01, 0x02, 0x03, 0x81, 0x82 and 0x83. | [`SighashType::parse`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L144) | none |
| From v5, a `SIGHASH_SINGLE` signature of an input without an output at the index of the input fails validation. hayai gives no digest, so the signature check returns false, as in zcashd, Zebra and Zakura; a script such as `<pk> OP_CHECKSIG OP_NOT` still passes in all four nodes. | [`SIGHASH_SINGLE`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L145) | [`sighash_single_needs_the_output_of_its_index_from_v5`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L834) |
| The shielded signature hash uses `SIGHASH_ALL`. | [`SignableInput::Shielded`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L125) | [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |
| The authorizing digest hashes the transparent scripts and the Sapling and Orchard authorizing digests with the personalization `ZTxAuthHash_` and the branch id. | [`auth_digest`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L436), [`AUTH_PREFIX`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L477) | [`upstream_transaction_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L111), [`auth_digest_follows_zip_239`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/vectors.rs#L195) |
| `hashAuthDataRoot` is the Merkle tree of the authorizing digests in txid order, padded with zero leaves, with the personalization `ZcashAuthDatHash`. | [`auth_data_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L388), [`auth_pair`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L336) | [`auth_data_root_padding_is_zero_leaves`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L602), [`auth_data_root_matches_sequential`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L552) |
| `hashBlockCommitments` is BLAKE2b-256 with the personalization `ZcashBlockCommit` of the history root, the authorizing data root and 32 zero bytes. | [`block_commitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L461), [`block_commitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L170) | [`block_commitments_is_personalized_blake2b_over_both_roots`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L584), [`nu5_and_later_commit_to_the_root_and_the_auth_data_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L323) |
| The change applies from the NU5 activation block. | [`block_commitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L170) | [`nu5_and_later_commit_to_the_root_and_the_auth_data_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L323) |
| A full node uses the full structure of the latest upgrade that it supports. | [`block_commitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L170) | [`nu5_and_later_commit_to_the_root_and_the_auth_data_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L323) |

## [ZIP 250](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0250): Deployment of the Heartwood Network Upgrade

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` of Heartwood is `0xF5B9230B`. | [`BranchId::Heartwood`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L528), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId::Heartwood`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L791) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of Heartwood is 903,800 on Testnet and 903,000 on Mainnet. | [`NetworkUpgrade::Heartwood`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L25), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Heartwood`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L496) (Mainnet), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Heartwood`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L531) (Testnet) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| A Heartwood node advertises at least the protocol version 170,010 on Testnet and 170,011 on Mainnet. | [`PROTOCOL_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L29) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| After the Heartwood activation, a node refuses new peers and disconnects the existing peers that have a version before Heartwood (recommendation). | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version 170,150 is above 170,011, so no peer before Heartwood completes the handshake) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484) |
| The minimum peer protocol version is 170,002. | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version is 170,150; no peer below it follows the chain after NU6.2) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| A signature uses the Heartwood consensus branch id. | [`PrepareError::BranchId`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L205), [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::ZCASH_SIGHASH_PERSONALIZATION_PREFIX`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash_v4.rs#L141), [`zcash_primitives::hash_header_txid_data`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/txid.rs#L229) | [`sighash_vector_transactions_through_draft`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_txs.rs#L267) |

## [ZIP 251](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0251): Deployment of the Canopy Network Upgrade

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` of Canopy is `0xE9FF75A6`. | [`BranchId::Canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L529), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId::Canopy`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L792) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of Canopy is 1,028,500 on Testnet and 1,046,400 on Mainnet. | [`NetworkUpgrade::Canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L26), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Canopy`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L497) (Mainnet), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Canopy`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L532) (Testnet), [`Network::mandatory_checkpoint_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L159) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988), [`the_mandatory_checkpoint_is_the_block_before_canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L274) |
| A Canopy node advertises at least the protocol version 170,012 on Testnet and 170,013 on Mainnet. | [`PROTOCOL_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L29) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| After the Canopy activation, a node refuses new peers and disconnects the existing peers that have a version before Canopy (recommendation). | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version 170,150 is above 170,013, so no peer before Canopy completes the handshake) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484) |
| The minimum peer protocol version is 170,002. | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version is 170,150; no peer below it follows the chain after NU6.2) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| A signature uses the Canopy consensus branch id. | [`PrepareError::BranchId`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L205), [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::ZCASH_SIGHASH_PERSONALIZATION_PREFIX`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash_v4.rs#L141), [`zcash_primitives::hash_header_txid_data`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/txid.rs#L229) | [`sighash_vector_transactions_through_draft`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_txs.rs#L267) |

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|

| `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of NU5 is 1,842,420 on Testnet (the second activation) and 1,687,104 on Mainnet. | [`NetworkUpgrade::Nu5`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L27), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Nu5`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L498) (Mainnet), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Nu5`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L533) (Testnet) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| `MIN_NETWORK_PROTOCOL_VERSION` of NU5 is 170,050 on Testnet and 170,100 on Mainnet. | [`Upgrade::Nu5`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L75), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version 170,150 is above both values) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| An NU5 node advertises at least the protocol version 170,050 on Testnet and 170,100 on Mainnet. | [`PROTOCOL_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L29) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| After the NU5 activation, a node refuses new peers and disconnects the existing peers that have a version before NU5 (recommendation). | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version 170,150 is above 170,100, so no peer before NU5 completes the handshake) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484) |
| An NU5 node accepts the v4 and the v5 transaction formats. | [`tx_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L224), [`PrepareError::Version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |
| The `MSG_WTX` change of ZIP 239 applies from the peer version 170,014. | [`SessionError::VersionTooOld`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L168), [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51) (the minimum peer version 170,150 is above 170,014, so every connection can use `MSG_WTX`) | [`handshake_errors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/session.rs#L484) |
