## [ZIP 256](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0256): Deployment of Consensus Bug Fixes Between NU6.1 and NU6.2

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The `rk` of an Orchard action is not the identity point. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::Action::from_parts`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L305), [`orchard::ActionFromPartsError::IdentityRk`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/action.rs#L65) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| The transparent pool and the deferred pool are not negative, and the total of the chain value pools is at most `MAX_MONEY` (change to ZIP 209). | [`ContextError::NegativeTransparentPool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L828), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L837), [`checked_sum`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L839) | [`each_value_pool_follows_its_change`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1379), [`the_value_pools_follow_the_block`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L700) |
| A node verifies the Sprout proofs when it connects a block. | [`joinsplit_proof_is_valid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L176), [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L242) (a BCTV14 proof has no verifier; the checkpoint list covers the blocks with these proofs) | [`the_embedded_key_verifies_the_published_joinsplits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L83), [`a_changed_joinsplit_transaction_fails`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L106) |
| The `ephemeralKey` of an Orchard action encodes a valid Pallas point that is not the identity. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::Action::from_parts`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L305), [`orchard::ActionFromPartsError::InvalidEpk`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/action.rs#L73) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| A v4 transaction with no Sapling spends and no Sapling outputs has `valueBalanceSapling` equal to 0. | [`PrepareError::V4ValueBalance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L404), [`v4_value_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/scan.rs#L193) | [`a_v4_value_balance_without_sapling_components_is_zero`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L985), [`v4_value_balance_is_recovered_from_the_wire`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L564) |
| From NU5, a change to the authorizing data of a block body does not make the header invalid. | [`ContextError::BlockCommitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node/fault.rs#L129), [`Fault::WrongBody`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node/fault.rs#L54) | [`each_error_has_the_fault_of_its_cause`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/node/fault.rs#L153) |
| The count of the P2SH input sigops is the count of zcashd. | [`sigops`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L326), [`last_push`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L310) | [`sigops_count_legacy_and_p2sh_like_zcashd`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1027), [`p2sh_pattern_is_exact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1062) |
| Each transparent input is checked against its own spent output. | [`resolve_inputs`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L287), [`ContextError::SpentMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L494) | [`prepared_against_a_different_coin_is_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L327) |
| A coinbase transaction has no Sapling spend. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseShieldedSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L439) | none (issue hayai-cvi) |
| The transparent inputs and the spent outputs are aligned before the script verification. | [`PrepareError::SpentCoins`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L462) | [`a_wrong_number_of_spent_coins_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L790) |
| A node does not take a v5 transaction as verified on its txid only. | [`PreparedStore::txs`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L162) (the store keys each verified transaction by its wtxid, which holds the authorizing data digest) | [`store_detects_conflicts_tracks_parents_and_streams_events`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L244) |

## [ZIP 257](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0257): Deployment of the Orchard Temporary Vulnerability Mitigation and NU6.2 Network Upgrade

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` of NU6.2 is `0x5437F330`. | [`BranchId::Nu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L533), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId::Nu6_2`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L796) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of NU6.2 is 4,052,000 on Testnet and 3,364,600 on Mainnet. | [`NetworkUpgrade::Nu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L30), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Nu6_2`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L501) (Mainnet), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Nu6_2`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L536) (Testnet) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988), [`the_orchard_pool_is_off_from_the_soft_fork_until_nu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L1271) |
| `MIN_NETWORK_PROTOCOL_VERSION` of NU6.2 is 170,150 on Mainnet and Testnet. | [`INITIAL_MIN_PEER_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L51), [`Upgrade::Nu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L78) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| An NU6.2 node advertises at least the protocol version 170,150 on Mainnet and Testnet. | [`PROTOCOL_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L29) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| The Orchard soft fork starts at the height 3,363,426 on Mainnet and 4,048,500 on Testnet. | [`orchard_disabled_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L711) (Mainnet), [`orchard_disabled_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L759) (Testnet) | [`the_orchard_pool_is_off_from_the_soft_fork_until_nu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L1271), [`the_orchard_pool_is_off_in_the_soft_fork_range`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L528) |
| From the start of the Orchard soft fork until NU6.2, a v5 or later transaction has no Orchard actions. | [`NU6_1_ORCHARD_DISABLED`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L349), [`ContextError::PoolNotActive`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L678) (block validation; the mempool uses the rule set of the branch) | [`the_orchard_pool_is_off_in_the_soft_fork_range`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L753) |
| From NU5 to NU6.1, an Orchard proof uses the verifying key `OrchardCircuitVersion::InsecurePreNU6_2`. | [`OrchardCircuitVersion::InsecurePreNu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L80), [`circuit_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L233) | [`circuit_version_follows_the_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L115) |
| In NU6.2, an Orchard proof uses the verifying key `FixedPostNU6_2`. | [`OrchardCircuitVersion::FixedPostNu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L81), [`circuit_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L233) | [`circuit_version_follows_the_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L115) |
| An Orchard proof is valid under the verifying key of the epoch. | [`circuit_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L233), [`verify_orchard`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L318), [`add_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L103), [`orchard::BatchValidator::validate`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L121) | [`bisection_isolates_the_tampered_orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L162), [`invalid_proofs_and_keys_fail_the_shielded_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L381) |
| From NU6.2, the length of `proofsOrchard` is 2,720 + 2,272 × the number of actions bytes. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::Bundle::try_from_parts`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L142), [`orchard::validate_proof_size`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle.rs#L489), [`orchard::Proof::expected_proof_size`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/lib.rs#L111) | [`a_proof_has_the_canonical_length`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1456) |
| From NU6.2, a transaction with Orchard actions is valid again. | [`NU6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L269) (the NU6.2 rule set has the Orchard pool) | [`the_orchard_pool_is_off_in_the_soft_fork_range`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L753) |

## [ZIP 258](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0258): Deployment of the NU6.3 Network Upgrade

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` of NU6.3 is `0x37A5165B`. | [`BranchId::Nu6_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L534), [`zcash_protocol::BranchId::Nu6_3`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L797) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of NU6.3 is 4,134,000 on Testnet and 3,428,143 on Mainnet. | [`NetworkUpgrade::Nu6_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L31), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Nu6_3`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L502) (Mainnet), [`zcash_protocol::NetworkUpgrade::Nu6_3`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_protocol/0.10.6/source/src/consensus.rs#L537) (Testnet) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988), [`activation_heights_match_zcash_protocol`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L943) |
| `MIN_NETWORK_PROTOCOL_VERSION` of NU6.3 is 170,160 on Mainnet and Testnet. | [`Upgrade::Nu6_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L64) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| An NU6.3 node advertises at least the protocol version 170,160 on Mainnet and Testnet. | [`PROTOCOL_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L29) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| A coinbase transaction has no Orchard-pool actions. | [`PrepareError::CoinbaseOrchardBundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L444) | [`the_coinbase_rules_of_the_orchard_and_ironwood_bundles`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1528) |
| Every Orchard-pool action in a v5 or v6 transaction has `enableCrossAddress` equal to 0, and the verifying key of the branch enforces it. | [`PrepareError::OrchardCrossAddress`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L421), [`OrchardCircuitVersion::PostNu6_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L82), [`orchard::BatchError::RestrictionUnsupportedByKey`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L84) | [`the_flag_bits_of_each_pool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1386), [`circuit_version_follows_the_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L115) |
| `valueBalanceOrchard` is at least 0, so no value enters the Orchard pool. | [`PrepareError::OrchardPoolDeposit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L427) | [`no_value_enters_the_orchard_pool_from_nu6_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1493), [`context_free_rules_reject_at_the_block_level`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L462) |
| The binding signature makes the Ironwood actions agree with `valueBalanceIronwood`. | [`add_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L103), [`orchard::binding_validating_key`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L97) | [`invalid_proofs_and_keys_fail_the_shielded_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L381) |
| `anchorIronwood` is the final Ironwood root of an earlier block. | [`ContextError::BadAnchor`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L562) | [`ironwood_anchors_are_roots_of_earlier_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L626) |
| The Ironwood note commitment tree has at most 2^32 leaves. | [`TreeError::Full`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/batch.rs#L67) | [`full_tree_is_an_error_not_a_panic`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/tests.rs#L144) |
| An Ironwood coinbase output has a note plaintext with the lead byte 0x03. | [`ironwood_action_decrypts`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L112), [`IronwoodDomain::for_action`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L67), [`orchard::NOTE_VERSION`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/note_encryption.rs#L98), [`orchard::NoteVersion::V3`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/note_encryption.rs#L123) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| ZIP 2005 activates at NU6.3. | [`ironwood`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L280), [`ironwood_action_decrypts`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L112) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448), [`an_ironwood_coinbase_output_decrypts_only_with_the_zero_key`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L347) |

## [ZIP 259](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0259): Deployment of the NU7 Network Upgrade

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| NU7 deploys ZIP 207 revision 2, ZIP 214 revision 3, ZIP 218, ZIP 235, ZIP 237, ZIP 2003 and ZIP 2008, and does not deploy ZIP 233 or ZIP 234. | [`NU7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L301) (each deployed ZIP has its own section) | [`the_rules_of_each_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L448) |
| From NU7 on Testnet, a block whose time is more than 18 target spacings (450 s) after its parent has `nBits` equal to ToCompact(PoWLimit). | [`min_difficulty_gap_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L490), [`DifficultyParams::POST_NU7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L118) | [`the_window_and_the_spacing_change_at_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L453) |
| `CONSENSUS_BRANCH_ID` of NU7 is `0x77190AD9`. | [`BranchId::Nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-crypto/src/lib.rs#L87), [`nu7_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L535) (the zakura backend only; the default upstream backend has no NU7 branch id and stops at the NU7 height) | [`nu7_is_known_to_the_zakura_backend_only`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-crypto/src/lib.rs#L183), [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of NU7 is 4,465,026 on Testnet, and Mainnet has no NU7 height yet. | [`TESTNET_HEIGHTS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L683), [`MAINNET_HEIGHTS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L652) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988), [`the_nu7_boundary_on_testnet`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/rules.rs#L242) |
| Each `ACTIVATION_HEIGHT` of NU7 is a multiple of 3. | [`TESTNET_HEIGHTS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L683) (a constant; hayai does not check a configured Regtest height) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988) |
| `NSM_REISSUANCE_HEIGHT` is 7,305,222 on Testnet, and ZIP 237 gives the Mainnet value. | [`reissuance_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/nsm.rs#L38) | [`the_nsm_values_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_nu7.rs#L276) |
| `MIN_NETWORK_PROTOCOL_VERSION` of NU7 is 170,180 on Testnet and 170,190 on Mainnet. | [`Upgrade::Nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L65) (Mainnet), [`Upgrade::Nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L66) (Testnet) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| An NU7 node advertises at least the protocol version 170,180 on Testnet and 170,190 on Mainnet. | [`PROTOCOL_VERSION_NU7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L34), [`protocol_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L41) (a build without the NU7 rule set advertises 170,160 and stops before NU7) | [`minimum_peer_version_follows_the_upgrade`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-net/src/protocol.rs#L265) |
| From NU7, a v4 transaction is not valid (ZIP 2003). | [`tx_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L304), [`PrepareError::Version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198) | [`a_v4_transaction_is_refused_from_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L905) |

## [ZIP 271](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0271): Dev Fund Extension and One-Time Disbursement

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The coinbase of the NU6.1 activation block has the lockbox disbursement outputs to the 2-of-3 P2SH multisig address. | [`lockbox::disbursements`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L216), [`disbursements`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L64) | [`each_required_output_needs_its_own_coinbase_output`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L242), [`the_disbursement_is_in_the_nu6_1_activation_block_only`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L92) |
| `ZIP271ActivationHeight` is the NU6.1 activation height. | [`Upgrade::Nu6_1`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L29), [`Upgrade::Nu6_1`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L29) | [`the_disbursement_is_in_the_nu6_1_activation_block_only`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L92) |
| `ZIP271DisbursementAmount` is the balance of the lockbox at the end of the block before the activation block. | [`nu6_1_disbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/lockbox.rs#L37) (a constant, as the ZIP note allows; the computed pool equals it on Mainnet and Testnet) | [`the_deferred_pool_of_a_chain_pays_the_disbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L366) |
| `ZIP271DisbursementChunks` is 10. | [`nu6_1_disbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/lockbox.rs#L37) | [`the_disbursement_is_in_the_nu6_1_activation_block_only`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L92) |
| `ZIP271DisbursementAddress` is t3ev37Q2… on Mainnet and t2RnBRiq… on Testnet. | [`MAINNET_ADDRESS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/lockbox.rs#L79), [`TESTNET_ADDRESS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/lockbox.rs#L81) | [`the_disbursement_is_in_the_nu6_1_activation_block_only`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L92) |
| The disbursement pays the amount in 10 equal outputs with the standard P2SH script of the multisig address (ZIP 48). | [`OutputKind::LockboxDisbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L189), [`p2sh_script`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L37) | [`each_required_output_needs_its_own_coinbase_output`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L242) |
| The disbursement amount enters the transparent value of the coinbase and leaves the lockbox. | [`CoinbaseTerms::payable`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L267), [`lockbox::deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L355) | [`terms_have_the_outputs_of_each_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L78), [`a_coinbase_with_every_required_output_and_the_exact_value_is_valid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L148) |
| The disbursement deduction comes before the other changes to the lockbox in the block and does not make the lockbox negative at that point. | [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L78) (the check is on the pool after the block, as in Spec §4.17; no Mainnet or Testnet block gives another result; issue hayai-28v) | [`the_deferred_pool_pays_the_disbursement_or_the_block_fails`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1493) |
| The disbursement amount is 78,750 ZEC on Mainnet and Testnet. | [`nu6_1_disbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/lockbox.rs#L37) | [`the_disbursement_is_in_the_nu6_1_activation_block_only`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L92) |
| The deferred pool is the sum of `totalDeferredOutput - totalDeferredInput` over the blocks (Spec §4.17). | [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L78), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L837) | [`the_deferred_pool_follows_the_terms`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L337) |
| A block that makes the deferred pool negative is not valid (Spec §4.17). | [`CoinbaseError::NegativeDeferredPool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L91), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L837) | [`the_deferred_pool_follows_the_terms`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L337), [`the_deferred_pool_pays_the_disbursement_or_the_block_fails`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1493) |
| The 100-block coinbase maturity applies to the disbursement outputs (Spec §7.1.2). | [`COINBASE_MATURITY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L501) | none |
| `totalDeferredInput(height)` is the disbursement amount at `ZIP271ActivationHeight` and 0 at every other height (Spec §7.8). | [`CoinbaseTerms::disbursed`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L235) | [`terms_have_the_outputs_of_each_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L78) |
| The coinbase has the funding stream outputs and the disbursement outputs (Spec §7.10). | [`OutputKind::LockboxDisbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L189), [`unmatched_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L369) | [`each_required_output_needs_its_own_coinbase_output`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L242), [`coinbases_of_the_block_vectors_pass_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L29) |
| The standard redeem script hash of a multisig address comes from ZIP 48, and older addresses do not change (Spec §7.10). | [`p2sh_script`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L37) | [`address_script_is_the_p2sh_script_of_the_address`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L146) |
| A funding output to a Sapling or Orchard address uses the prescribed way (Spec §7.10). | Not implemented: no funding stream and no disbursement has a Sapling or Orchard recipient, so the rule has no effect on the current networks. | none |
| Equal funding items need distinct outputs, one output for each item. | [`CoinbaseTerms::check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L315) | [`each_required_output_needs_its_own_coinbase_output`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L242), [`a_configured_regtest_pays_its_disbursements_at_nu6_1`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L523) |
| The revision 2 funding stream tables of ZIP 214 apply (Spec §7.10.1). | [`lockbox_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L145) (Mainnet), [`lockbox_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L145) (Testnet) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480), [`testnet_streams_have_their_ranges_and_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L513) |
| `FS_CCF_H3` continues the 12 % lockbox stream, and ZIP 214 revision 3 ends it at the third halving. | [`numerator`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L408), [`ends_at_third_halving`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L553), [`nu7_adjusted_end`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L94) | [`the_last_testnet_streams_follow_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L559) |
| The Key-Holders make a new multisig address after a key loss. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder organizations, not a node. | none |

## [ZIP 317](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0317): Proportional Transfer Fee Mechanism

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The conventional fee is `marginal_fee * max(grace_actions, logical_actions)` (recommendation for wallets). | [`Zip317Params::conventional_fee`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L51) | [`conventional_fee_applies_grace_actions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L163) |
| The `marginal_fee` is 5,000 zatoshis. | [`marginal_fee`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L45) (hayai uses 400 zatoshis, the value of Zakura, so that the node relays and mines the same transactions as Zakura) | [`unpaid_actions_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L909) |
| The `grace_actions` value is 2. | [`grace_actions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L46) | [`grace_actions_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L953) |
| The `p2pkh_standard_input_size` value is 150 bytes. | [`P2PKH_STANDARD_INPUT_SIZE`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L99) | [`logical_actions_count_transparent_bytes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L208) |
| The `p2pkh_standard_output_size` value is 34 bytes. | [`P2PKH_STANDARD_OUTPUT_SIZE`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L100) | [`logical_actions_count_transparent_bytes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L208), [`the_policy_cases_of_the_regtest_pair_have_the_verdict_of_zakura`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L1024) |
| The transparent part of the logical actions is `max(ceil(tx_in_total_size / 150), ceil(tx_out_total_size / 34))`. | [`P2PKH_STANDARD_INPUT_SIZE`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L123), [`P2PKH_STANDARD_OUTPUT_SIZE`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L124) | [`logical_actions_count_transparent_bytes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L208), [`the_policy_cases_of_the_regtest_pair_have_the_verdict_of_zakura`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L1024) |
| The Sprout part of the logical actions is `2 * nJoinSplit`. | [`sprout_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L125) | none |
| The Sapling part of the logical actions is `max(nSpendsSapling, nOutputsSapling)`. | [`sapling_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L128) | none |
| The Orchard part of the logical actions is `nActionsOrchard`. | [`orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L134) | [`shielding_tx_is_valid_and_follows_the_policy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/admission.rs#L424) |
| From NU6.3 (revision 1), the Ironwood part of the logical actions is `nActionsIronwood`. | [`ironwood_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L137) | [`a_template_candidate_counts_its_ironwood_actions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L807) |
| The memo chunk part of the logical actions is `max(0, nMemoChunks - free_memo_chunks)` (revision 2, draft). | Not implemented: the rule needs ZIP 231 and ZIP 248, and no network upgrade deploys them. | none |
| A wallet creates transactions that pay the conventional fee (recommendation). | Not implemented: hayai creates no wallet transaction. | none |
| A node drops a transaction that has more unpaid actions than a limit (option). | [`unpaid_action_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L395) (the limit is 0) | [`unpaid_actions_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/policy.rs#L909) |
| The `low_fee_penalty` threshold of ZIP 401 is the conventional fee. | [`conventional_fee`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L362), [`LOW_FEE_PENALTY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L372) | [`an_entry_holds_the_zip_317_and_zip_401_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L661) |
| The `weight_ratio_cap` value is 4. | [`weight_ratio_cap`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L47) (hayai uses 13, the value of Zakura; the value changes the order of the template, not a verdict) | [`weight_ratio_is_capped_and_ordered`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L172) |
| The `block_unpaid_action_limit` value is 50. | [`BLOCK_UNPAID_ACTION_LIMIT`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L21) (hayai uses 0, the value of Zakura and Zebra) | [`the_unpaid_action_limit_of_zero_bounds_the_second_pass`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1362) |
| The unpaid actions are `max(0, max(grace_actions, logical_actions) - floor(fee / marginal_fee))`, and 0 for a coinbase. | [`Zip317Params::unpaid_actions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L70), [`InsertError::Coinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L332) (a coinbase is never a candidate) | [`unpaid_actions_follow_the_marginal_fee`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L197) |
| The `block_unpaid_actions` value is the sum of the unpaid actions of the transactions of the block. | [`unpaid_actions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L270) | [`the_unpaid_action_limit_of_zero_bounds_the_second_pass`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1362) |
| Step 1 of block production starts with a coinbase placeholder and reserves its size and its sigops. | [`Budget::fresh`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L248) | [`incremental_add_matches_from_scratch_under_a_tight_byte_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1220), [`the_limits_are_the_limits_of_the_rule_set_of_the_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1274) |
| Step 2 of block production computes `weight_ratio = min(max(1, fee) / conventional_fee, weight_ratio_cap)`. | [`weight_ratio_cap`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L64) (fixed-point arithmetic) | [`weight_ratio_is_capped_and_ordered`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/zip317.rs#L172) |
| Step 3 of block production picks each candidate that pays the conventional fee at random by weight ratio, and adds it when the block stays in the size and sigop limits. | [`LiveTemplate::walk`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L841), [`OrderKey`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/candidate.rs#L109) (the pick follows the order of the weight ratio, not a random draw, so the same set gives the same template on every node) | [`random_event_histories_match_from_scratch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1638), [`child_is_selected_only_after_its_parents`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1144) |
| Step 4 of block production adds the other candidates in the same way, also in the limit of unpaid actions. | [`LiveTemplate::walk`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L841), [`OrderKey`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/candidate.rs#L109), [`unpaid_actions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L270) (the same order as step 3) | [`the_unpaid_action_limit_of_zero_bounds_the_second_pass`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1362) |
| Steps 3b and 4b of block production keep the block in the block size limit and the block sigop limit. | [`size_bytes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L268), [`sigops`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L269) | [`incremental_add_matches_from_scratch_under_a_tight_byte_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1220), [`the_limits_are_the_limits_of_the_rule_set_of_the_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1274) |
| A node that builds block templates uses the block production algorithm of the ZIP (recommendation). | [`LiveTemplate::walk`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L841), [`OrderKey`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/candidate.rs#L109) (the pick follows the order of the weight ratio, not a random draw) | [`random_event_histories_match_from_scratch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1638), [`child_is_selected_only_after_its_parents`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L1144) |
| Mempool and relay restrictions do not start before the change of the block template algorithm (recommendation). | Not implemented: the rule sets the deployment order of 2023, which is complete. | none |

## [ZIP 401](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0401): Addressing Mempool Denial-of-Service

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The cost of a transaction is `max(memory size, 10,000)`, and a node can use the serialized size as the memory size. | [`MEMPOOL_COST_THRESHOLD`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L361) | [`an_entry_holds_the_zip_317_and_zip_401_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L661) |
| The eviction weight is the cost plus 40,000 when the fee is below the conventional fee of ZIP 317. | [`conventional_fee`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L362), [`LOW_FEE_PENALTY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L372) | [`an_entry_holds_the_zip_317_and_zip_401_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L661) |
| A node keeps a FIFO list `RecentlyEvicted` of (txid, time) entries, keyed by txid and not by wtxid. | [`RecentlyEvicted`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L96), [`InsertError::RecentlyEvicted`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L339) | [`an_evicted_transaction_is_refused_until_the_memory_expires`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L789) |
| The `RecentlyEvicted` list is empty at the start of the node (recommendation). | [`RecentlyEvicted::new`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L221) (the list is in memory only) | none |
| The `RecentlyEvicted` list holds at most 40,000 entries (recommendation). | [`EVICTION_MEMORY_ENTRIES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L66), [`capacity`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L133) | [`the_recently_evicted_list_is_bounded`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L827) |
| A node has the setting `mempooltxcostlimit`, with the default value 80,000,000. | [`tx_cost_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/config.rs#L504), [`MEMPOOL_TX_COST_LIMIT`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L55) | [`the_output_of_zakurad_generate_gives_an_exact_report`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/config.rs#L1542) |
| A node has the setting `mempoolevictionmemoryminutes`, with the default value 60. | Not implemented: open, issue hayai-6iw. [`EVICTION_MEMORY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L64) fixes the time at 60 min, and the operator cannot change it. | none |
| A node drops a received transaction whose txid is in `RecentlyEvicted`. | [`is_recently_evicted`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/admission.rs#L175), [`InsertError::RecentlyEvicted`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L339) | [`an_evicted_transaction_is_refused_until_the_memory_expires`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L789) |
| When the total cost with the new transaction is above the limit, the node calls `EvictTransaction` again until the cost fits, and the new transaction is a candidate. | [`cost_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L376) | [`the_total_cost_is_never_above_the_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L753) |
| `EvictTransaction` selects a transaction at random, with a probability in proportion to its eviction weight. | [`PreparedStore::select_victim`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L412), [`gen_range`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L426) | [`eviction_follows_the_eviction_weights`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L712) |
| The node adds the txid and the time of the evicted transaction to `RecentlyEvicted`, and removes the oldest entry from a full list. | [`RecentlyEvicted::add`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L383), [`capacity`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L133) | [`the_recently_evicted_list_is_bounded`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L827) |
| The node removes the selected transaction from the mempool. | [`remove_entry`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L382), [`ancestors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L374) (the descendants of the victim leave with it, and an ancestor of the new transaction is not a candidate) | [`eviction_removes_the_descendants_of_the_victim`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L934), [`eviction_never_selects_an_ancestor_of_the_new_transaction`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L892) |
| The node removes the entries older than `mempoolevictionmemoryminutes` from `RecentlyEvicted` (recommendation). | [`EVICTION_MEMORY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L114) | [`an_evicted_transaction_is_refused_until_the_memory_expires`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-mempool/src/store.rs#L789) |

## [ZIP 1014](https://zips.z.cash/zip-1014): Establishing a Dev Fund for ECC, ZF, and Major Grants

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| From the first halving to the second halving, 20 % of the block subsidy goes to the Dev Fund: 35 % of it to BP, 25 % to ZF and 40 % to MG. | [`MAINNET`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L189) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480) |
| Testnet has the same streams (recommendation). | [`TESTNET`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L270) | [`testnet_streams_have_their_ranges_and_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L513) |
| ZF receives and administers the MG slice. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| ZF disburses the MG funds for Major Grants and for their administration. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| The MG funds go only to independent grantees and to administration. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| The MG funds do not pay the internal operations of ZF. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| The Community Advisory Panel selects the Major Grant Review Committee. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| A member of the Committee has a term of one year. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| A member of the Committee does not take part in a decision in which the member has a financial interest. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| ZF operates the Community Advisory Panel. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| The Panel decides the amount of the Discretionary Budget. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| The Committee approves each payment from the Discretionary Budget. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| ZF keeps the MG slice as a Restricted Fund. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| ZF defines target metrics. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| With the direct-grant option, ZF publishes the addresses of the grantees, and ECC and ZF add them to the code. | Not implemented: nobody used the option, and ZIP 214 sets no such address. | none |
| BP, ECC, ZF and the grantees accept the obligations of the ZIP. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| They disclose conflicts of interest and the listed facts promptly. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| They disclose security and privacy risks. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| BP, ECC and ZF accept the conditions in a contract. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |
| A member of the board of ZF holds no equity of ECC. | Not implemented: the rule binds the organizations that receive the Dev Fund, not a node. | none |

## [ZIP 1015](https://zips.z.cash/zip-1015): Block Subsidy Allocation for Non-Direct Development Funding

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| 12 % of the block subsidy goes to the lockbox. | [`Receiver::Deferred`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L407) | [`the_deferred_part_is_the_lockbox_stream`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L241) |
| 8 % of the block subsidy goes to FPF for ZCG. | [`Receiver::MajorGrants`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L412) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480) |
| On Mainnet, the streams run from height 2,726,400 to height 3,146,400. | [`lockbox_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L213) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480) |
| On Testnet, the streams run from height 2,976,000 to height 3,396,000. | [`lockbox_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L294) | [`testnet_streams_have_their_ranges_and_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L513) |

## [ZIP 1016](https://zips.z.cash/zip-1016): Community and Coinholder Funding Model

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The ZCG stream of 8 % runs from the end of `FS_FPF_ZCG` to height 4,406,400, or to `A + 3 * (4,406,400 - A)` after an NU7 activation at height `A`. | [`lockbox_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L219), [`nu7_adjusted_end`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L94) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480), [`only_an_end_above_the_nu7_height_moves`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L487) |
| The Coinholder-Controlled Fund gets the contents of the lockbox (ZIP 271) and a stream of 12 % for the same period. | [`Receiver::Deferred`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L407), [`disbursements`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L64) | [`the_deferred_part_is_the_lockbox_stream`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L241), [`the_deferred_pool_of_a_chain_pays_the_disbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L366) |
| A legal agreement binds the Key-Holder Organizations. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| The rules of ZIP 1015 for the use of the lockbox apply to the fund. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| A coinholder vote needs 420,000 ZEC. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| The payments of a vetoed grant stop. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| A veto has a rationale (recommendation). | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| The provisions of ZIP 1015 for ZCG continue. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| The organizations keep the funds safe. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| An organization reports a loss of funds or a compromised key. | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |
| A rehearsal of the voting process occurs on Testnet (recommendation). | Not implemented: the rule binds the Key-Holder Organizations and the coinholders, not a node. | none |

## [ZIP 2001](https://zips.z.cash/zip-2001): Lockbox Funding Streams

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| Each element of `fs.Recipients` is a P2SH address, a Sapling address, or `DEFERRED_POOL`. | [`Receiver::Deferred`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L407) | [`the_deferred_stream_has_no_address`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L788), [`every_address_is_a_p2sh_address_of_its_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L771) |
| A full node keeps the balance `ChainValuePoolBalance^Deferred`. | [`CoinbaseTerms::deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L355), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L78), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L837) | [`the_deferred_pool_follows_the_terms`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L337), [`the_deferred_pool_of_a_chain_pays_the_disbursement`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L366) |
| `totalDeferredOutput(height)` is the sum of the values of the `DEFERRED_POOL` streams. | [`deferred_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L370), [`deferred_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L370) | [`the_deferred_part_is_the_lockbox_stream`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L241) |
| The coinbase output rule applies to each active stream other than `DEFERRED_POOL`. | [`Subsidy::deferred`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L209) | [`terms_have_the_outputs_of_each_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L78), [`from_nu6_the_value_is_exact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L263) |
| The recipient of a stream is `fs.Recipient(height) = fs.Recipients[fs.RecipientIndex(height)]`. | [`address_period`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L261) | [`the_ecc_address_changes_at_each_period_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L674) |
| `IssuedSupply` is the sum of the chain value pools, with the deferred pool. | [`ValuePools::total`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/lib.rs#L132), [`check_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L865) | [`the_nsm_rules_apply_from_the_block_before_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1528) |
| The total output value of the coinbase is its transparent outputs minus `vbalanceSapling` and `vbalanceOrchard` plus `totalDeferredOutput`, and its total input value is the block subsidy plus the fees. | [`CoinbaseTerms::check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L338), [`CoinbaseTerms::payable`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L267) | [`value_that_enters_a_shielded_pool_is_paid_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L316), [`from_nu6_the_value_is_exact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L263) |
| Before NU6 the total output value of the coinbase is at most its total input value, and from NU6 it is equal to it (with ZIP 236). | [`exact_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L337), [`CoinbaseError::ValueAboveLimit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L345) | [`from_nu6_the_value_is_exact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L263), [`before_nu6_the_value_is_a_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L298) |

|---|---|---|

| Decryption derives `esk`, `rcm` and `psi` from `rseed` and `rho` with the new rules. | [`IronwoodDomain::for_action`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L67), [`orchard::NoteVersion::V3`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/note.rs#L390), [`orchard::rcm_v3`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/note.rs#L203) (OVK recovery of coinbase outputs) | [`an_ironwood_coinbase_output_decrypts_only_with_the_zero_key`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/coinbase.rs#L347) |
| Wallets follow the rules for key derivation (`qsk`, `qk`), FROST and hardware wallets. | Not implemented: the rules apply to wallets, not to a node. | none |

## [Protocol specification §3.8](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#notecommitmenttrees): Note commitment trees

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A block does not add Sprout note commitments past the capacity of 2^29 leaves. | [`TreeError::Full`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/sprout.rs#L121), [`append_leaves`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L889) | [`a_full_tree_takes_no_leaf`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/sprout.rs#L293) |
| From Sapling, a block does not add Sapling note commitments past the capacity of 2^32 leaves. | [`TreeError::Full`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/batch.rs#L67) | [`a_full_sapling_tree_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/tests.rs#L166) |
| From NU5, a block does not add Orchard note commitments past the capacity of 2^32 leaves. | [`TreeError::Full`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/batch.rs#L67) | [`full_tree_is_an_error_not_a_panic`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/tests.rs#L144) |
| From NU6.3, a block does not add Ironwood note commitments past the capacity of 2^MerkleDepth^Orchard leaves. | [`IronwoodFrontier`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/lib.rs#L100), [`TreeError::Full`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/batch.rs#L67) (the Ironwood tree is the Orchard tree type) | [`full_tree_is_an_error_not_a_panic`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-trees/src/tests.rs#L144) |

## [Protocol specification §3.9](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#nullifierset): Nullifier sets

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A nullifier does not occur two times in a transaction or in the chain, and the Sprout, Sapling, Orchard and Ironwood nullifier sets are separate. | [`ContextError::DuplicateNullifier`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L526), [`ChainView::contains_nullifier_many`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L546), [`NullifierStore`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-coins/src/nullifiers.rs#L122), [`PrepareError::DuplicateNullifier`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L528), [`ContextError::DuplicateNullifier`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check/checkpoint.rs#L65) | [`duplicate_nullifiers_are_rejected_in_view_and_in_block`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L338), [`a_sprout_nullifier_is_revealed_once`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L578), [`ironwood_nullifiers_are_unique_in_the_block_the_layers_and_the_base`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L571), [`the_checkpoint_path_refuses_a_nullifier_twice_in_the_block`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/checkpoint.rs#L391), [`nullifier_sets_are_per_pool_and_persist_on_flush`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-coins/tests/store.rs#L235) |

## [Protocol specification §3.11](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#coinbasetransactions): Coinbase transactions

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| Before Canopy, the coinbase transaction pays the founders' reward (§7.9). | [`founders_reward`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L188), [`unmatched_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L369), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_missing_required_output_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L172), [`coinbases_of_the_block_vectors_pass_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L29) |
| From Canopy, the coinbase transaction pays the funding streams (§7.10). | [`funding_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L199), [`unmatched_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L369) | [`a_missing_required_output_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L172), [`coinbases_of_the_block_vectors_pass_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L29) |

## [Protocol specification §4.3](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#joinsplitdesc): JoinSplit descriptions

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The elements of a JoinSplit description have their types, and `vpub_old` and `vpub_new` are in the range 0 to MAX_MONEY. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::from_u64_le_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sprout.rs#L94), [`zcash_primitives::from_u64_le_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sprout.rs#L102) | none |
| The JoinSplit proof is valid (Groth16 from Sapling, BCTV14 before Sapling). | [`verify_proof`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L139), [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L243) (BCTV14 proofs: the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_wrong_proof_is_not_valid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L343), [`the_embedded_key_verifies_the_published_joinsplits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L83), [`a_changed_joinsplit_transaction_fails`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L106), [`a_bctv14_joinsplit_is_an_error_and_a_wrong_groth16_joinsplit_fails`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L423) |
| In each JoinSplit description, `vpub_old` or `vpub_new` is zero. | [`PrepareError::JoinSplitBothVpub`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L92) | [`one_of_vpub_old_and_vpub_new_is_zero`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1801) |
| From Canopy, `vpub_old` is zero. | [`PrepareError::SproutPoolDeposit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L95) | [`no_value_enters_the_sprout_pool_from_canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1817) |

## [Protocol specification §4.4](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#spenddesc): Spend descriptions

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The elements of a Spend description are valid encodings of their types. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::read_spend_v4`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L223), [`zcash_primitives::read_spend_v5`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L265) | none |
| `cv` and `rk` of a Spend description are not of small order. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::from_bytes_not_small_order`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L148), [`check_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L91), [`sapling_crypto::is_small_order`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier.rs#L49) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
| The Spend proof is valid. | [`validate`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L95), [`sapling_crypto::verify_proofs`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier/batch.rs#L157) | [`the_embedded_keys_verify_a_proof_of_the_official_parameters`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L136), [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |
| `spendAuthSig` is a valid signature over the shielded signature hash, with a canonical `R`. | [`check_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L91), [`sapling_crypto::spend_auth_sig`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier/batch.rs#L75), [`sapling_crypto::verify`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier/batch.rs#L142) | [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |

## [Protocol specification §4.5](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#outputdesc): Output descriptions

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The elements of an Output description are valid encodings of their types. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::read_output_v4`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L280), [`zcash_primitives::read_output_v5`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L344) | none |
| `cv` and `epk` of an Output description are not of small order. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::from_bytes_not_small_order`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L148), [`check_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sapling.rs#L91), [`sapling_crypto::is_small_order`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/verifier.rs#L108) | none (issue hayai-8od) |
