## [Protocol specification §4.6](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#actiondesc): Action descriptions

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The elements of an Action description are canonical encodings of their types. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::read_action_without_auth`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L298), [`zcash_primitives::Action::from_parts`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L305) | none |
| `spendAuthSig` of an Action description is a valid signature over the shielded signature hash, with a canonical `R`. | [`add_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L103), [`orchard::create_batch_item`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L93) | [`bisection_isolates_the_tampered_orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L162) |
| From NU5 to NU6.1, the verifying key `vk` is `InsecurePreNU6_2`. | [`OrchardCircuitVersion::InsecurePreNu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L80) | [`circuit_version_follows_the_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L115) |
| In NU6.2, the verifying key `vk` is `FixedPostNU6_2`. | [`OrchardCircuitVersion::FixedPostNu6_2`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L81) | [`circuit_version_follows_the_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L115) |
| From NU6.3, the verifying key `vk` is `PostNU6_3`. | [`OrchardCircuitVersion::PostNu6_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L82) | [`circuit_version_follows_the_branch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L115) |
| The Action proof is valid for `vk` with the primary input (cv, rt, nf, rk, cmx, enableSpends, enableOutputs). | [`ScopedBatch::add`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L216), [`ScopedBatch::finalize`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L286), [`orchard::add_to_batch`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L106), [`orchard::finalize`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L138) | [`bisection_isolates_the_tampered_orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L162), [`invalid_proofs_and_keys_fail_the_shielded_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L381) |
| `rk` of an Action description is not the identity point. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::Action::from_parts`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L305), [`orchard::is_identity`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/action.rs#L65) | none (issue hayai-8od) |

## [Protocol specification §4.10](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#sighash): SIGHASH transaction hashing

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| Orchard-pool actions occur only in a transaction of version 5 or later. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::orchard_bundle`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L821) (the version 4 format has no Orchard field) | none |
| Ironwood-pool actions occur only in a transaction of version 6. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::ironwood_bundle`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L873) (the version 5 format has no Ironwood field) | none |
| From version 5, the hash type of a transparent signature is a canonical value (0x01 to 0x03, 0x81 to 0x83). | [`SighashType::parse`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L144), [`zcash_transparent::parse`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_transparent/0.10.0/source/src/sighash.rs#L34) | none (issue hayai-cvi) |
| Version 1 and version 2 transactions use the signature hash of ZIP 76. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L211), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_version_before_sapling_is_not_verified`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1891) |
| Version 3 transactions use the signature hash of ZIP 143. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L211), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_version_before_sapling_is_not_verified`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1891) |
| Version 4 transactions use the signature hash of ZIP 243. | [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::v4_signature_hash`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash.rs#L48) | [block vectors](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/vectors/expected-blocks.json), [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |
| Version 5 transactions use the signature hash of ZIP 244. | [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::v5_signature_hash`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash.rs#L51) | [block vectors](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/vectors/expected-blocks.json), [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |
| Version 6 transactions use the signature hash of ZIP 229. | [`signature_hash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L163), [`zcash_primitives::v6_signature_hash`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/sighash.rs#L53) | [`a_nu6_3_block_validates_cold_and_warm_to_identical_layers`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L159) |
| In each epoch, every transaction uses the consensus branch id of the epoch (Overwinter 0x5BA81B19, Sapling 0x76B809BB, Blossom 0x2BB40E60, Heartwood 0xF5B9230B, Canopy 0xE9FF75A6, NU5 0xC2D6D0B4, NU6 0xC8E71055, NU6.1 0x4DEC4DF0, NU6.2 0x5437F330, NU6.3 0x37A5165B). | [`Upgrade::branch_id`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L522), [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`PrepareError::BranchId`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L205) (Overwinter: the checkpoint list covers these heights; the text of §4.10 gives 0xF919A198 for NU5, issue hayai-27o) | [`the_deployment_constants_of_the_zips`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L988), [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682), [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |

## [Protocol specification §4.17](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#chainvaluepoolbalances): Chain value pool balances

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The transparent chain value pool (the value of the UTXO set) does not become negative. | [`ContextError::NegativeTransparentPool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L828) | [`no_value_pool_can_be_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1413) |
| The Sprout chain value pool does not become negative. | [`sprout_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L830), [`ContextError::NegativeValuePool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L822) | [`no_value_pool_can_be_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1413), [`the_sprout_pool_is_never_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L615) |
| The Sapling chain value pool does not become negative. | [`sapling_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L831), [`ContextError::NegativeValuePool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L822) | [`no_value_pool_can_be_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1413) |
| The Orchard chain value pool does not become negative. | [`orchard_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L832), [`ContextError::NegativeValuePool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L822) | [`no_value_pool_can_be_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1413) |
| From NU6.1, the deferred development fund pool (the sum of `totalDeferredOutput` minus `totalDeferredInput`) does not become negative. | [`CoinbaseTerms::deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L837), [`deferred_pool_after`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L355) | [`the_deferred_pool_pays_the_disbursement_or_the_block_fails`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1493) |
| From NU6.3, the Ironwood chain value pool does not become negative. | [`ironwood_balance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L834), [`ContextError::NegativeValuePool`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L822) | [`no_value_pool_can_be_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1413), [`the_ironwood_pool_does_not_go_negative`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L677) |
| `IssuedSupply` (the sum of the six chain value pools, without overflow) is at most MAX_MONEY. | [`checked_sum`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L839), [`MAX_MONEY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L238) | [`the_total_of_the_pools_is_bounded`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1617) |

## [Protocol specification §7.1](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#txnencoding): Transaction encoding and consensus

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| When `effectiveVersion` is 5 or more, the parser reads the rest of the transaction in the version 5 format. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::read_v5`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L744) | [`upstream_transaction_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L111) |
| The rules of each JoinSplit, Spend, Output and Action description apply to the transaction. | [`check_joinsplits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L83), [`ScopedBatch::add`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/shielded.rs#L216) (the rows of §4.3 to §4.6 and §7.2 to §7.5 give each rule) | [`block_vectors_match_the_expected_outcomes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_blocks.rs#L593) |
| Before Overwinter, a transaction version above 2 has the rules of version 2. | [`TxVersion::Sprout`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L192), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_version_before_sapling_is_not_verified`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1891) |
| Mainnet and Testnet do not use the version 0x7FFFFFFF and the version group id 0xFFFFFFFF. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::TxVersion::read`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L82) | none |

## [Protocol specification §7.1.1](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#txnidentifiers): Transaction identifiers

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The transaction id of a transaction of version 4 or earlier is the SHA-256d hash of its encoding. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::from_data_v4`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L697) | [`merkle_root_matches_header`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/vectors.rs#L49) |
| The transaction id of a version 5 transaction is the digest of ZIP 244, and of a version 6 transaction the digest of ZIP 229. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::to_txid`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L709), [`zcash_primitives::to_txid`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L719) | [`upstream_transaction_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L111) |
| A transaction of version 5 or later has a wtxid (ZIP 239). | [`RawTx::wtxid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L96) | [`wtxid_bytes_are_txid_then_digest`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L609) |

## [Protocol specification §7.1.2](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#txnconsensus): Transaction consensus rules

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The version number of a transaction is 1 or more. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::TxVersion::Sprout`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L98) | none |
| Before Overwinter, the `fOverwintered` flag of a transaction is not set. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L211), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_version_before_sapling_is_not_verified`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1891) |
| From Overwinter, the `fOverwintered` flag of a transaction is set. | [`PrepareError::Version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198) | [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682) |
| From Overwinter, the version group ID of a transaction is a known value. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::TxVersion::read`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L82) | none |
| In the Overwinter epoch, a transaction has version 3 and the version group ID 0x03C48270. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L211), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_version_before_sapling_is_not_verified`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1891) |
| From Sapling to Canopy, a transaction has version 4. | [`PrepareError::Version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198), [`SAPLING`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L184) (the checkpoint list covers the blocks before Canopy) | [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682) |
| From NU5 to NU6.2, a transaction has version 4 or 5. | [`PrepareError::Version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198), [`NU5`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L220) | [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682) |
| From NU6.3, a transaction has version 4, 5 or 6. | [`PrepareError::Version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L198), [`NU6_3`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L275) | [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682) |
| A version 4 transaction has the version group ID 0x892F2085. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::V4_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L90) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| A version 5 transaction has the version group ID 0x26A7270A. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::V5_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L91) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| A version 6 transaction has the version group ID 0xD884B698. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::V6_VERSION_GROUP_ID`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/mod.rs#L92) | [`generated_transactions_of_every_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/scan.rs#L499) |
| From version 5, the `nConsensusBranchId` field of a transaction equals the consensus branch ID of the epoch. | [`PrepareError::BranchId`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L205) | [`the_rule_set_names_the_transaction_versions`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1682), [`a_v4_transaction_is_refused_from_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L905) |
| Before Sapling, the size of a transaction is at most 100,000 bytes. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L211), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | none |
| From NU5, `nSpendsSapling`, `nOutputsSapling` and `nActionsOrchard` are each less than 2^16. | [`MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L289) (a block has at most 2,000,000 bytes, and 2^16 spends, outputs or actions need more than 6 MB) | [`oversized_block_is_rejected_before_parsing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L620) |
| From NU6.3, `nActionsIronwood` of a version 6 transaction is less than 2^16. | [`MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L289) (a block has at most 2,000,000 bytes, and 2^16 actions need more than 50 MB) | [`oversized_block_is_rejected_before_parsing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L620) |
| Before Sapling, a version 1 transaction or a transaction without JoinSplits has transparent inputs and transparent outputs. | [`PrepareError::Unsupported`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L211), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326), [`CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L472) (the checkpoint list covers these heights) | none |
| From Sapling, a transaction before version 5 has a transparent input, a Sapling spend or a JoinSplit. | [`PrepareError::NoSource`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L389) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292), [`a_joinsplit_is_a_source_and_a_sink`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1780) |
| From Sapling, a transaction before version 5 has a transparent output, a Sapling output or a JoinSplit. | [`PrepareError::NoSink`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L399) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292), [`a_joinsplit_is_a_source_and_a_sink`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1780) |
| A version 5 transaction has a transparent input, a Sapling spend, or Orchard actions with `enableSpendsOrchard` set. | [`PrepareError::NoSource`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L389) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292) |
| A version 5 transaction has a transparent output, a Sapling output, or Orchard actions with `enableOutputsOrchard` set. | [`PrepareError::NoSink`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L399) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292) |
| A version 6 transaction has a source of funds, and Ironwood actions with `enableSpendsIronwood` set count as a source. | [`PrepareError::NoSource`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L389) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292) |
| A version 6 transaction has a sink of funds, and Ironwood actions with `enableOutputsIronwood` set count as a sink. | [`PrepareError::NoSink`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L399) | [`a_source_and_a_sink_of_funds_respect_the_enable_flags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1292), [`context_free_rules_reject_at_the_block_level`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L462) |
| Orchard actions have `enableSpendsOrchard` or `enableOutputsOrchard` set. | [`PrepareError::OrchardFlags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L411) | [`actions_need_an_enable_flag`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1355) |
| Ironwood actions have `enableSpendsIronwood` or `enableOutputsIronwood` set. | [`PrepareError::IronwoodFlags`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L414) | [`actions_need_an_enable_flag`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1355) |
| From Mainnet height 3,363,426 or Testnet height 4,048,500 until NU6.2, a transaction has no Orchard actions. | [`CoreSpec::orchard_disabled`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L345), [`ContextError::PoolNotActive`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L678) | [`the_orchard_pool_is_off_in_the_soft_fork_range`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L753) |
| A transaction that spends a coinbase output has no transparent outputs. | [`ContextError::UnshieldedCoinbaseSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L514) | [`immature_coinbase_spend_is_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L263), [`mature_coinbase_spent_to_shielded_outputs_is_accepted`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L301), [`regtest_allows_a_coinbase_spend_with_transparent_outputs`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L531), [`a_real_transaction_meets_the_context_rules_at_their_boundaries`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/mempool.rs#L66) |
| `joinSplitPubKey` is a valid encoding of an Ed25519 public key. | [`VerificationKey::try_from`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/sprout.rs#L166), [`ed25519_zebra::Error::MalformedPublicKey`](https://docs.rs/crate/ed25519-zebra/4.2.0/source/src/verification_key.rs#L164) | none |

| `bindingSigOrchard` is a valid signature. | [`add_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L103), [`orchard::binding_validating_key`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L98) | [`bisection_isolates_the_tampered_orchard_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L162) |
| `bindingSigIronwood` is a valid signature. | [`add_bundle`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/orchard.rs#L103), [`orchard::binding_validating_key`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/bundle/batch.rs#L98) | [`invalid_proofs_and_keys_fail_the_shielded_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L381) |

## [Protocol specification §7.3](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#spendencodingandconsensus): Spend description encoding and consensus

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `anchorSapling`, when present, is less than q_J. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::read_base`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L168), [`zcash_primitives::read_base`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L464) | none (issue hayai-8od) |

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `cmu` is less than q_J. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::ExtractedNoteCommitment::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/sapling.rs#L162), [`sapling_crypto::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/sapling-crypto/0.7.0/source/src/note/commitment.rs#L72) | none (issue hayai-8od) |

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `cmx` is less than q_P. | [`Transaction::read`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L121), [`zcash_primitives::ExtractedNoteCommitment::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_primitives/0.30.1/source/src/transaction/components/orchard.rs#L275), [`orchard::from_bytes`](https://docs.rs/crate/orchard/0.15.5/source/src/note/commitment.rs#L87) | none (issue hayai-8od) |

## [Protocol specification §7.6](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#blockheader): Block header encoding and consensus

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The block version, read as a signed 32-bit integer, is at least 4. | [`MIN_BLOCK_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L29), [`check_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L450), [`check_version`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L450) | [`a_real_header_with_one_broken_rule_fails_that_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L115) |
| `nBits` equals `ThresholdBits(height)`. | [`expected_bits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L158) (Regtest has no difficulty rule) | [`a_real_header_with_one_broken_rule_fails_that_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L115), [`bits_are_checked_or_reported_as_unchecked`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L340), [`generated_chains_match_the_reference`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L291), [`a_chain_from_genesis_matches_the_reference`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L337) |
| The block passes the difficulty filter (§7.7.2). | [`check_pow`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/header.rs#L188), [`PowError::HashAboveTarget`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L284) | [`a_real_header_with_one_broken_rule_fails_that_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L115), [`check_pow_compares_little_endian`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L558) |
| `solution` is a valid Equihash solution (§7.7.1). | [`check_equihash`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/header.rs#L190), [`is_valid_solution`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L298), [`equihash::is_valid_solution`](https://docs.rs/crate/equihash/0.3.0/source/src/verify.rs#L243) | [`a_real_header_with_one_broken_rule_fails_that_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L115), [`the_first_real_blocks_pass_every_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L70) |
| The `nTime` of each block other than the genesis block is greater than the median-time-past. | [`HeaderRuleError::TimeTooEarly`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L126) | [`the_time_rules_and_their_start_heights`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L258), [`the_median_time_past_reads_eleven_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L313) |
| `nTime` is at most the median-time-past plus 90 · 60 s, from height 2 on Mainnet and from height 653,606 on Testnet. | [`max_time_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L135), [`max_time_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L709), [`max_time_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L757) | [`the_time_rules_and_their_start_heights`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L258) |
| A block has at most 2,000,000 bytes. | [`MAX_BLOCK_BYTES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L289) | [`oversized_block_is_rejected_before_parsing`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L620) |
| In Sapling and Blossom, `hashLightClientRoot` is the final Sapling root of the block. | [`BranchId::Blossom`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L163), [`ContextError::BlockCommitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L961) | none |
| In Heartwood and Canopy, `hashLightClientRoot` is `hashChainHistoryRoot` (ZIP 221). | [`BranchId::Canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L167), [`ContextError::BlockCommitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L961) | [`mainnet_heartwood_activation`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L219), [`mainnet_canopy_activation_resets_the_tree`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L277) |
| From NU5, the header field is `hashBlockCommitments` (ZIP 244). | [`block_commitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/history.rs#L170), [`ContextError::BlockCommitments`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L961) | [`nu5_and_later_commit_to_the_root_and_the_auth_data_root`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/tests/history.rs#L323), [`tampered_blocks_fail_at_the_right_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/validate.rs#L83) |
| A block has at least one transaction. | [`ParseError::Empty`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/lib.rs#L297) | [`malformed_blocks_are_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/tests/vectors.rs#L221) |
| The first transaction of a block is a coinbase transaction, and no other transaction of the block is a coinbase transaction. | [`ContextError::NoCoinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L384), [`ContextError::ExtraCoinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1002) | [`coinbase_placement_and_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L787) |
| Other rules come from Bitcoin (an open item of the specification). | Not implemented: the specification names no rule here, and the section of block rules without a ZIP holds the rules that hayai takes from Bitcoin. | none |
| A full validator does not accept a block whose `nTime` is more than 2 h after the clock of the node. | [`HeaderRuleError::TimeTooFarAhead`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L67), [`check_local_time`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/header.rs#L129), [`check_local_time`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-node/src/sync.rs#L123) (the header check applies it before block validation) | [`the_local_time_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L524) |
| A miner does not create a block with a version other than 4. | [`BLOCK_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/live.rs#L58), [`BLOCK_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-template/src/publisher.rs#L239), [`BLOCK_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-rpc/src/template.rs#L125) | none |
| A block with a version above 4 has the rules of a version 4 block. | [`MIN_BLOCK_VERSION`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L29) (the check accepts every version from 4 to 0x7FFFFFFF) | [`a_real_header_with_one_broken_rule_fails_that_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L115) |
| `solutionSize` has the minimal CompactSize encoding, and the parser refuses every other encoding. | [`CompactSize::read_t`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L119), [`zcash_encoding::CompactSize::read`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_encoding/0.4.0/source/src/lib.rs#L33) | [`parse_rejects_short_unknown_and_non_canonical_lengths`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L634) |

## [Protocol specification §7.7](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#pow): Proof of work

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| Mainnet and Testnet use Equihash with n = 200 and k = 9, and a solution has 1344 bytes. | [`PowParams::MAINNET`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L50), [`PowParams::TESTNET`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L52), [`PowParams::MAINNET`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L703), [`HeaderRuleError::SolutionLength`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/header.rs#L170) | [`mainnet_and_testnet_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L1217), [`known_parameter_sets_have_the_specified_lengths`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L625), [`a_real_header_with_one_broken_rule_fails_that_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L115) |
| The Equihash solution satisfies the generalized birthday and algorithm binding conditions over the header fields up to `nBits` and the nonce `nNonce`, with 21-bit big-endian indices. | [`equihash::is_valid_solution`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L298), [`equihash::is_valid_solution`](https://docs.rs/crate/equihash/0.3.0/source/src/verify.rs#L243) | [`the_first_real_blocks_pass_every_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L70), [`a_real_header_with_one_broken_rule_fails_that_rule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L115) |
| The SHA-256d hash of the whole header, read as a little-endian integer, is at most `ToTarget(nBits)`. | [`PowError::HashAboveTarget`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L284) | [`check_pow_compares_little_endian`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L558), [`check_target_applies_the_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L533) |
| `median(S)` is the element of `sorted(S)` at the 1-based index ceiling((len + 1) / 2). | [`median_time`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L422) | [`median_is_the_upper_middle_element`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L641) |
| `MedianTime(h)` is the median of the `nTime` values of the 11 blocks before `h`, or of all of them when fewer exist. | [`median_time_past`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L439), [`median_time_past`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L119) | [`the_median_time_past_reads_eleven_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/header.rs#L313), [`median_is_the_upper_middle_element`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L641) |
| `MeanTarget(h)` is the mean of `ToTarget(nBits)` of the `PoWAveragingWindow` blocks before `h`. | [`mean_target`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L517), [`mean_target`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L552) | [`mean_target_is_the_floor_of_the_exact_mean`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L706), [`zakura_vector_of_a_mixed_window`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L398) |
| `ActualTimespanDamped` truncates with `PoWDampingFactor` 4, and `ActualTimespanBounded` keeps it between 84 % and 132 % of `AveragingWindowTimespan`. | [`bounded_timespan`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L584), [`bounded_timespan`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L584) | [`timespan_bounds_of_both_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L658), [`the_clamp_limits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L526) |
| `Threshold(h)` is min(PoWLimit, floor(MeanTarget / AveragingWindowTimespan) · ActualTimespanBounded), `ThresholdBits` is its compact form, and `nBits` equals `ThresholdBits`. | [`Uint256::checked_mul_u64`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L532), [`compact_from_u256`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/difficulty.rs#L37), [`HeaderRuleError::WrongBits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/header_rules.rs#L160) | [`the_result_is_at_most_the_pow_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L554), [`generated_chains_match_the_reference`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L291) |
| `MeanTarget(h)` is PoWLimit when `h` is at most `PoWAveragingWindow`. | [`NetworkParams::pow_limit_bits`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L503) (the threshold is PoWLimit at these heights, as in zcashd; the specification gives `ActualTimespan` no value there, issue hayai-8sc) | [`the_result_is_at_most_the_pow_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L554), [`the_first_blocks_of_both_networks_have_the_limit`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L723) |
| On Testnet from height 299,188, a block more than 6 target spacings after its parent has the minimum difficulty (ZIP 205, ZIP 208), and Mainnet has no such rule. | [`DifficultyParams::min_difficulty_gap_spacings`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L490), [`NetworkParams::min_difficulty_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L754), [`NetworkParams::min_difficulty_start_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L708) | [`testnet_minimum_difficulty_at_the_gap_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L584), [`testnet_minimum_difficulty_blocks_of_the_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/tests/difficulty.rs#L750) |
| `ToCompact(x)` gives the compact form of a target. | [`compact_from_target`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L230), [`compact_from_u256`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/difficulty.rs#L37) | [`compact_vectors_of_bitcoin_and_zcashd`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L444), [`compact_round_trip`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L374), [`compact_truncates_to_the_top_bytes`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L385), [`compact_and_u256_round_trip`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/difficulty.rs#L107) |
| `ToTarget(x)` is 0 when the sign bit is set, else the mantissa times 256^(exponent − 3). | [`expand_target`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L192), [`target_from_compact`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/difficulty.rs#L30) (a zero or overflowing target fails the header, as in zcashd `CheckProofOfWork`; a valid `nBits` equals `ThresholdBits`, so the result is the same) | [`expand_target_matches_known_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L404), [`compact_vectors_of_bitcoin_and_zcashd`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-wire/src/header.rs#L444) |
| The work of a block is floor(2^256 / (ToTarget(nBits) + 1)). | [`block_work`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/difficulty.rs#L44) | [`block_work_vectors`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/difficulty_rules.rs#L742) |
| The best chain is the valid chain with the greatest total work. | [`block_work`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L486), [`HeaderChain::push`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L519) (on equal work the first header that arrives stays the best tip) | [`best_tip_is_the_most_work_not_the_most_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/tests/headers.rs#L130), [`equal_work_keeps_the_first_seen_tip`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/tests/headers.rs#L168) |

## [Protocol specification §7.8](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#subsidies): Calculating block subsidy, funding streams, lockbox disbursement, and founders' reward

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| Below `SlowStartShift` the subsidy is `SlowStartRate · h`, and from `SlowStartShift` to `SlowStartInterval` it is `SlowStartRate · (h + 1)`. | [`block_subsidy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L67), [`scheduled_issuance`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L28) | [`mainnet_subsidy_follows_the_schedule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L69), [`the_scheduled_issuance_is_the_sum_of_the_subsidies`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L196) |
| `Halving(height)` counts the halvings before and after Blossom. | [`halving`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L22) | [`halvings_and_subsidies_without_the_nu7_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L118), [`schedules_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L268) |
| `BlockSubsidy(height)` is the slow start value, then floor(MaxBlockSubsidy / (BlossomRatio · 2^Halving(height))). | [`MAX_BLOCK_SUBSIDY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L37), [`MAX_BLOCK_SUBSIDY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L37) | [`mainnet_subsidy_follows_the_schedule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L69), [`testnet_subsidy_follows_the_schedule`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L90), [`halvings_and_subsidies_without_the_nu7_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L118) |
| `FoundersReward(height)` is `BlockSubsidy(height) · FoundersFraction` while `Halving(height)` is below 1. | [`FOUNDERS_FRACTION_DIVISOR`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L29), [`FOUNDERS_FRACTION_DIVISOR`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L29) | [`the_reward_is_a_fifth_of_the_subsidy_until_canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L123) |
| `fs.Value(height)` is 0 before Canopy, floor(BlockSubsidy(height) · fs.Numerator / fs.Denominator) in the range of the stream, and 0 outside it. | [`Upgrade::Canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/chain_spec.rs#L26), [`DENOMINATOR`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L49) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480), [`testnet_streams_have_their_ranges_and_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L513), [`the_last_testnet_streams_follow_nu7`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L559), [`schedules_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L268) |
| `totalDeferredOutput(height)` is the sum of the values of the `DEFERRED_POOL` streams. | [`deferred_value`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L370), [`Subsidy::deferred`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L209) | [`the_deferred_part_is_the_lockbox_stream`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/subsidy.rs#L241) |
| `totalDeferredInput(height)` is the disbursement amount at `ZIP271ActivationHeight`, and 0 at every other height. | [`lockbox::disbursements`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L216), [`disbursements`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L64) | [`terms_have_the_outputs_of_each_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L78) |
| `MinerSubsidy(height)` is `BlockSubsidy(height)` minus `FoundersReward(height)` minus the sum of `fs.Value(height)`. | [`CoinbaseTerms::miner_subsidy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L179) | [`terms_have_the_outputs_of_each_era`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L78) |

## [Protocol specification §7.9](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#foundersreward): Payment of founders' reward

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `FounderAddressList` holds 48 Mainnet addresses and 48 Testnet addresses. | [`MAINNET_ADDRESSES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L58), [`TESTNET_ADDRESSES`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L113) | [`every_address_is_a_p2sh_address_of_its_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L236), [`schedules_match_zakura_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L268) |
| `FounderAddressChangeInterval` is ceiling((SlowStartShift + PreBlossomHalvingInterval) / 48), which is 17,709. | [`change_interval`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L68) | [`the_address_changes_every_17709_adjusted_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L193) |
| From Blossom, `FounderAddressAdjustedHeight(h)` is Blossom + floor((h − Blossom) / 2). | [`adjusted_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L71) | [`the_address_changes_every_17709_adjusted_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L193) |
| `FounderAddressIndex` is 1 + floor(FounderAddressAdjustedHeight / FounderAddressChangeInterval). | [`founders_reward`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L81) (the index starts at 0) | [`the_address_changes_every_17709_adjusted_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L193) |
| `FoundersRewardLastBlockHeight` is the last height whose `Halving` is below 1 (1,046,399 from Blossom). | [`subsidy::halving`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L49) | [`the_reward_is_a_fifth_of_the_subsidy_until_canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L123) |
| `FounderRedeemScriptHash(height)` is the standard redeem script hash of the founder address of the height. | [`p2sh_script`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L37) | [`address_script_is_the_p2sh_script_of_the_address`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L146) |
| `FounderAddressIndex(FoundersRewardLastBlockHeight)` is at most `NumFounderAddresses`. | [`founders_reward`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/founders.rs#L81) (the index is below 48 at every height before the first halving) | [`the_address_changes_every_17709_adjusted_blocks`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/founders.rs#L193) |
| Before Canopy, a coinbase at a height from 1 to `FoundersRewardLastBlockHeight` has an output of exactly `FoundersReward(height)` to `OP_HASH160 FounderRedeemScriptHash(height) OP_EQUAL`. | [`founders::founders_reward`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L188), [`CoinbaseTerms::check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L793), [`check_above_mandatory_checkpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L326) (every height with a founders' reward is at or below the mandatory checkpoint, so the checkpoint list covers these heights) | [`a_missing_required_output_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L172), [`coinbases_of_the_block_vectors_pass_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L29) |

## [Protocol specification §7.10](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#fundingstreams): Payment of funding streams, deferred lockbox, and lockbox disbursement

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A funding stream has a numerator, a denominator, a start height, an end height and a list of recipients. | [`Stream`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L57), [`StreamSet`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L71) | [`mainnet_values_match_the_zakura_test_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L480), [`testnet_streams_have_their_ranges_and_values`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L513) |
| `HeightForHalving`, `FSRecipientChangeInterval` and `FSRecipientPeriod` set the address period of a height. | [`address_period`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L261), [`PERIODS_PER_HALVING_INTERVAL`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L53), [`address_period`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L261), [`halving_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/subsidy_schedule.rs#L195) | [`the_first_halving_height_is_the_first_height_with_halving_one`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L748), [`the_ecc_address_changes_at_each_period_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L674) |
| `fs.RecipientIndex(height)` selects `fs.Recipient(height)` from the `fs.NumRecipients` recipients of the stream. | [`FundingStream::address`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L473) | [`the_ecc_address_changes_at_each_period_boundary`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L674) |
| `fs.Recipients` has `fs.NumRecipients` elements. | [`ChainSpecError::StreamAddresses`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L356), [`RegtestConfigError::StreamAddresses`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/network.rs#L325) (a list of one repeated address is one entry, and a Regtest list can be longer; the outputs are the same) | [`each_range_has_the_periods_of_the_zakura_address_counts`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L722) |
| Each recipient is a P2SH address, a Sapling address or `DEFERRED_POOL`. | [`p2sh_script`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L37), [`address_script`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L68) | [`every_address_is_a_p2sh_address_of_its_network`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L771) |
| A funding stream is active at a height when `fs.Value(height)` is above 0. | [`funding_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/funding.rs#L298) (a stream is active in its height range, as ZIP 207 says; only a configured Regtest stream after about 26 halvings has a value of 0 in its range) | [`a_subsidy_of_zero_has_no_funding_stream`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/funding.rs#L669) |
| From Canopy, the coinbase has one output of `fs.Value(height)` to the address of each active stream other than `DEFERRED_POOL`, in the prescribed way. | [`funding::funding_streams`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L199), [`CoinbaseTerms::check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L793) | [`a_missing_required_output_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L172), [`a_required_output_with_another_amount_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L205), [`a_required_output_with_another_script_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L224), [`coinbases_of_the_block_vectors_pass_the_coinbase_check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/conformance_subsidy.rs#L29) |
| From NU6.1, the coinbase at `ZIP271ActivationHeight` has `ZIP271DisbursementChunks` equal outputs to `ZIP271DisbursementAddress` with a total of `ZIP271DisbursementAmount`. | [`lockbox::disbursements`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L216), [`Disbursement::count`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L224), [`disbursements`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L64) (a block without a subsidy has no required output; only a Regtest network reaches this case) | [`each_required_output_needs_its_own_coinbase_output`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L242), [`the_disbursement_is_in_the_nu6_1_activation_block_only`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lockbox.rs#L92) |
| The prescribed way to pay a P2SH address is the script `OP_HASH160 fs.RedeemScriptHash(height) OP_EQUAL`. | [`OP_HASH160`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L11) | [`address_script_is_the_p2sh_script_of_the_address`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/address.rs#L146), [`a_required_output_with_another_script_is_an_error`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L224) |
| The prescribed way to pay a Sapling or Orchard address follows ZIP 213. | Not implemented: no funding stream and no disbursement has a Sapling or Orchard recipient. | none |
| Equal funding stream or disbursement payments need one distinct output each. | [`CoinbaseTerms::check`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/coinbase_value.rs#L315) | [`each_required_output_needs_its_own_coinbase_output`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L242), [`a_configured_regtest_pays_its_disbursements_at_nu6_1`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/coinbase.rs#L523) |

## [Protocol specification §7.11](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#scripts): Changes to the script system

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| `OP_CODESEPARATOR` is disabled and does not change the signature hash. | [`Raw::eval`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L606), [`zcash_script::Error::Disabled`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_script/0.6.0/source/src/opcode/mod.rs#L1260), [`zcash_script::OP_CODESEPARATOR`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_script/0.6.0/source/src/opcode/mod.rs#L1384) | none |

## [Protocol specification §7.12](https://zips.z.cash/protocol/protocol.pdf#bips): Bitcoin improvement proposals

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| BIP 16 (P2SH) applies from the genesis block. | [`RuleEpoch::CONSENSUS_FLAGS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/lib.rs#L80), [`SCRIPT_FLAGS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L132) | [`every_fixture_transaction_prepares`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prepared.rs#L39) |
| BIP 30 applies: no transaction overwrites an unspent transaction with the same txid. | Not implemented: BIP 34 heights and the NU5 coinbase expiry make each coinbase txid unique, and each other transaction spends an input or reveals a nullifier, so no new transaction can have the txid of an unspent one (issue hayai-wz8). | none |
| BIP 65 (`CHECKLOCKTIMEVERIFY`) applies from the genesis block. | [`RuleEpoch::CONSENSUS_FLAGS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/lib.rs#L80), [`SCRIPT_FLAGS`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/rule_sets.rs#L132) | none |
| BIP 66 (strict DER signatures) applies from the genesis block. | [`Raw::eval`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L606), [`zcash_script::Signature::is_valid_encoding`](https://docs.rs/crate/zcash_script/0.6.0/source/src/signature.rs#L373) (the upstream interpreter checks the DER encoding without a flag) | none |
| BIP 34 applies: the coinbase script starts with the block height, except in the genesis block. | [`height_push`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L396) | [`coinbase_placement_and_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L787) |
| BIP 11, BIP 14, BIP 31, BIP 35, BIP 37, BIP 61 and BIP 111 apply as network and standardness rules. | Not implemented: these BIPs set network and standardness rules, not block rules; the ZIP 201 and ZIP 204 sections hold the network rules of a node. | none |

## Checkpoints

hayai uses the checkpoint lists of Zakura: the files `main-checkpoints.txt` and `test-checkpoints.txt` of `crates/zakura-chain/src/parameters/checkpoint/` at Zakura revision `13779158253cfe315f73eadffb9b4c93c25e82a5`, copied to `crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints/`. Mainnet has 14,385 checkpoints up to height 3,499,045, and Testnet has 10,059 checkpoints up to height 4,023,200. The mandatory checkpoint is the last block before Canopy (Mainnet 1,046,399, Testnet 1,028,499). Full validation refuses a block at or below it, so such a block has only the checkpoint path. The rules of transaction versions 1 to 3 therefore apply only through the checkpoint list.

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| A header at a checkpoint height has the hash of the checkpoint. | [`RejectReason::CheckpointMismatch`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L471) | [`checkpoints_fix_the_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/tests/headers.rs#L577) |
| The checkpoint lists are the lists of Zakura: 14,385 Mainnet checkpoints up to height 3,499,045 and 10,059 Testnet checkpoints up to height 4,023,200. | [`MAINNET`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L41), [`TESTNET`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L44), [`main`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/build.rs#L11) | [`the_embedded_lists_are_the_source_files`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L221), [`the_source_files_are_the_files_of_the_zakura_clone`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L241) |
| A block at or below the mandatory checkpoint, the last block before Canopy, is accepted only through the checkpoint path. | [`BlockError::BelowMandatoryCheckpoint`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L328), [`mandatory_checkpoint_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L159), [`apply_checkpointed`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L359) | [`the_checkpoint_path_accepts_only_the_checkpointed_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/checkpoint.rs#L205), [`the_mandatory_checkpoint_is_the_block_before_canopy`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus/src/checkpoints.rs#L274) |
| No branch leaves the best chain below the finalized height, which is the last checkpoint or the tip height minus 1,000. | [`finalized_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L435), [`RejectReason::ForkBelowFinalized`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/src/headers.rs#L479) | [`branches_below_the_finalized_height_are_removed_and_refused`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-sync/tests/headers.rs#L518) |

## Block rules without a ZIP

| Rule | Code | Test |
|---|---|---|
| The parent of a block is the tip of the chain, and the block height is the parent height plus 1 (Spec §7.6 `hashPrevBlock`, zcashd `ConnectBlock`). | [`ContextError::WrongParent`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L352), [`ChainError::NotOnTip`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/lib.rs#L649), [`ChainError::NotOnTip`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/lib.rs#L575) | [`double_spend_across_blocks_is_a_missing_input`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L159), [`the_checkpoint_path_accepts_only_the_checkpointed_chain`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/checkpoint.rs#L205), [`push_rejects_a_layer_off_the_tip`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/tests.rs#L196) |
| The first transaction of a block is a coinbase transaction, and no other transaction is a coinbase transaction (zcashd `bad-cb-missing`, `bad-cb-multiple`). | [`ContextError::NoCoinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L384), [`ContextError::ExtraCoinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1002), [`ContextError::ExtraCoinbase`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1163) | [`coinbase_placement_and_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L787), [`a_first_transaction_that_is_not_a_coinbase_is_refused`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/prebuilt.rs#L221) |
| A block does not contain the same txid two times (CVE-2012-2459, zcashd `CheckBlock` `bad-txns-duplicate`). | [`ContextError::DuplicateTxid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1098), [`ContextError::DuplicateTxid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1170), [`ContextError::DuplicateTxid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L1274), [`duplicate_txid`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-validate/src/lib.rs#L317) | [`coinbase_placement_and_height`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L787), [`a_body_with_a_txid_twice_is_a_duplicate_txid_on_the_full_path`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/validate.rs#L166), [`a_body_with_a_txid_twice_is_a_duplicate_txid_on_the_checkpoint_path`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/checkpoint.rs#L336) |
| Each transparent input spends a coin that exists and is unspent in the chain (Spec §3.4 and §7.1.2). | [`ContextError::MissingInput`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L333), [`resolve_inputs`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L287) | [`double_spend_across_blocks_is_a_missing_input`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L159), [`tampered_blocks_fail_at_the_right_stage`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/validate.rs#L83) |
| A block does not spend the same outpoint two times (Spec §3.4 and §7.1.2). | [`ContextError::DoubleSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L481), [`ContextError::DoubleSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check/checkpoint.rs#L70) | [`double_spend_within_a_block_is_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L184), [`the_checkpoint_path_keeps_the_checks_that_guard_the_state`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/checkpoint.rs#L269) |
| An input spends an output of the same block only when the transaction of that output comes earlier in the block (Spec §3.4). | [`ContextError::MissingInput`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L488) (on the checkpoint path, the checkpoint hash covers the order) | [`spending_a_later_transaction_of_the_block_is_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L243) |
| A transaction does not spend a coinbase output that is less than 100 blocks old (Spec §7.1.2). | [`COINBASE_MATURITY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L501), [`COINBASE_MATURITY`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-consensus-core/src/lib.rs#L61) | [`immature_coinbase_spend_is_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L263), [`regtest_allows_a_coinbase_spend_with_transparent_outputs`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L531) |
| On Mainnet and Testnet, a transaction that spends a coinbase output has no transparent outputs; Regtest does not have this rule (Spec §7.1.2). | [`ContextError::UnshieldedCoinbaseSpend`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L514) | [`immature_coinbase_spend_is_rejected`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L263), [`regtest_allows_a_coinbase_spend_with_transparent_outputs`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L531) |
| A block has at most 20,000 signature operations, legacy and P2SH together (zcashd `ConnectBlock` `MAX_BLOCK_SIGOPS`). | [`ContextError::TooManySigops`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L694), [`sigops`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L326) | [`a_block_has_at_most_20_000_sigops`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/state.rs#L509), [`sigops_count_legacy_and_p2sh_like_zcashd`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-prepared/src/prepare.rs#L1027) |
| The note commitments of a block go to the note commitment trees in block order (Spec §3.4). | [`append_trees`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L872), [`append_leaves`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-state/src/check.rs#L889) | [`the_sprout_tree_gives_the_published_final_roots`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/sprout.rs#L168), [`a_nu6_3_block_validates_cold_and_warm_to_identical_layers`](https://github.com/zodl-inc/hayai/blob/main/crates/hayai-bench/tests/ironwood.rs#L159) |
| A transaction does not overwrite the unspent outputs of an earlier transaction with the same txid (BIP 30, zcashd `ConnectBlock` `bad-txns-BIP30`). | Not implemented: the coinbase script of each block starts with its height, so no new transaction can repeat the txid of a transaction with unspent outputs (issue hayai-wz8). | none |

## ZIPs that do not apply to a node

- [ZIP 224](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0224) (Orchard Shielded Protocol): the ZIP has no rule of its own. Protocol specification §4.6 and §7.5 hold the Orchard rules.
- [ZIP 233](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0233) and [ZIP 234](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0234) (Network Sustainability Mechanism: burn and issuance smoothing): NU7 does not deploy them ([ZIP 259](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0259)).
- [ZIP 254](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0254) (Deployment of the NU7 Network Upgrade): withdrawn. ZIP 259 replaces it.
- [ZIP 301](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0301) (Zcash Stratum Protocol): hayai has no Stratum server. It serves `getblocktemplate` and its own template push protocol (`docs/protocol-template-push.md`).
- [ZIP 323](https://zips.z.cash/zip-0323) (Specification of getblocktemplate for Zcash): reserved, with no text.
- [ZIP 2006](https://zips.z.cash/zip-2006) (Restricting Transfers into the Orchard Pool): reserved. ZIP 229 and ZIP 258 hold its rules.
- Wallet, address and light client ZIPs: 32, 48, 129, 173, 302, 304, 305, 307, 308, 311, 312, 314, 315, 316, 318, 320, 321, 322, 324, 325, 326, 332, 339, 350, 374, 400, 402, 416, and draft-ecc-authenticated-reply-addrs.
- Process and informational ZIPs: 0, 1, 2, 300, 306, 310, 319, 403, draft-ecc-onchain-accountable-voting, draft-mcgee-keyholders-organizations and draft-str4d-orchard-balance-proof.
- Consensus drafts that no network upgrade activates: 68, 112, 113, 222, 226, 227, 228, 231, 245, 248, 2002, 2004 and draft-arya-dairaemma-disable-addition-of-transparent-chain-value.
- Network draft that no node deploys: draft-arya-jvff-p2p-quic-transport.
- Withdrawn and obsolete ZIPs: 210, 220, 230, 246, 303, 313, and 1001 to 1013.
- Reserved ZIPs with no text: 76, 217, 219, 240, 260, 270, 309 and 2007. The rules of ZIP 76 (signatures before Overwinter) apply only to blocks below the mandatory checkpoint.

## Open points

- hayai-pzt: from NU7, [ZIP 2008](https://zips.z.cash/zip-2008) pays a part of the Mainnet `FS_FPF_ZCG_H3` funding stream to a P2PKH address. hayai has no code for this rule, because Mainnet has no NU7 height yet. The test `zip_2008_has_no_code_while_mainnet_has_no_nu7_height` fails when Mainnet gets a height.
- hayai-28v: ZIP 271 checks the deferred lockbox pool after the disbursement and before the deferred part of the same block. hayai, Zakura and specification §4.17 check the pool after the block. No Mainnet or Testnet block gives a different result.
- hayai-k6y: the start state of a shadow node has no history tree. The node therefore does not check `hashBlockCommitments` (ZIP 221, ZIP 244), also after an upgrade starts a new tree.
- hayai-wz8: hayai has no BIP 30 check. No input reaches the rule, because each txid commits to the height of a coinbase or to an outpoint or a nullifier that only that transaction spends.
- hayai-00t: hayai drops an `addrv2` entry of TORV3, I2P or CJDNS with a wrong length. ZIP 155 refuses the message. Zakura drops the entry too.
- hayai-eea: hayai accepts an inventory entry of type 0 (`MSG_ERROR`). ZIP 204 refuses it. Zakura accepts it too.
- hayai-b63: the misbehaviour points differ from the table of ZIP 204. hayai gives 50 points and a disconnect for each decode fault and for each message before the handshake.
- hayai-6iw: hayai has no setting for the eviction memory time of ZIP 401 (`mempoolevictionmemoryminutes`).
- hayai-cvi: no hayai test gives these refused inputs: a v5 transaction with a non-canonical hash type, a coinbase with a Sapling spend, a `valueBalance` out of range for each pool. The parser or the upstream crates enforce the rules.
- hayai-8od: no hayai test gives these refused inputs: an Orchard `rk` at the identity, an invalid Orchard `ephemeralKey`, a Sapling `rk` or `epk` of small order, a `cmx`, `cmu` or anchor out of range, the edge cases of ZIP 215 and ZIP 216, a wrong lead byte in an Orchard or Ironwood coinbase output. The upstream crates enforce the rules.
- hayai-xya: the published ZIP 143, ZIP 243 and ZIP 244 signature hash vectors do not run against `SighashContext` of hayai-prepared, because that type is private. The block vectors and the Zakura comparisons check the signature hash through script verification.

The specification text has 2 errors:

- hayai-27o: §4.10 gives the NU5 consensus branch id 0xF919A198. ZIP 252 defines 0xC2D6D0B4, and every NU5 block of Mainnet and Testnet carries it. hayai, zcashd and Zakura use 0xC2D6D0B4.
- hayai-8sc: §7.7.3 gives no threshold for a height at or below `PoWAveragingWindow` (17 blocks, 102 blocks from NU7), because `MedianTime(h − PoWAveragingWindow)` reads an empty list there. zcashd, Zakura and hayai use `PoWLimit`. On Mainnet and Testnet these heights are below the mandatory checkpoint.
